### [184] [Towards Precision Therapy in Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Clinical-Reasoning LLM for Risk Stratification and Treatment Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08602)
*Peng Cui,Jitao Wang,Siyan Xue,Yao Huang,Haoming Xia,Dong Li,Dengxiang Liu,Weilin Wang,Liping Liu,Leida Zhang,Yunfu Cui,Tao Peng,Daolin Ji,Haitao Zhao,Wei Zhang,Xiaojuan Wang,Weijie Ma,Zongren Ding,Jinlong Li,Yuan Ding,Jiajing Zhao,Zhiyu Chen,Chengkun Yang,Ziyue Huang,Jiaqi Liu,Fusheng Liu,Yang Zhou,Xiaojuan Wang,Zhongquan Sun,Shiyun Bao,Xiaojun Wang,Ming Yang,Guangxin Li,Bin Shu,Yong Liao,Hongxuan Li,Yao Tang,Shizhong Yang,Yongyi Zeng,Yufeng Yuan,Yinpeng Dong,Jihui Hao,Jun Zhu,Jiahong Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HCC-STAR是一个针对肝细胞癌（HCC）的临床决策支持大语言模型，能够从电子病历文本中联合输出基于风险评分的分期、符合指南的治疗推荐及个体化生存预测，在多中心队列中表现优于GPT-5和Gemini-2.5 Pro，并获得临床医生高度认可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HCC分期系统（如BCLC、CNLC）分类粗粒度，无法捕捉分期内的异质性，且难以整合电子病历中的临床上下文信息；当前LLM在临床决策支持中缺乏对指南的知识对齐推理和可验证性，亟需一个能综合分期、治疗和预后预测的精准决策系统。

Method: 基于约30,000例SEER HCC病例，经临床医生验证的提示式增强流程生成EMR风格叙事训练数据；构建知识对齐推理框架，采用步进可验证的复合奖励进行强化学习优化，超越对指南的文本级记忆。模型联合输出分期、排序的治疗方案（含循证依据）和个体化生存估计。

Result: 在12家中国医院共6,668例患者的多中心队列中，HCC-STAR在治疗推荐和风险分层上达到SOTA，超越GPT-5和Gemini-2.5 Pro及现有指南；假设性生存分析显示遵循HCC-STAR推荐的中位生存期为51个月，优于BCLC（29个月）和CNLC（32个月）；盲法肝胆专科医生评价其推理和循证依据可信；在治疗准确性上超越住院医师和主治医师，并作为辅助工具帮助医生更快做出更准确决策。

Conclusion: HCC-STAR被验证为HCC风险分层和精准治疗中可靠且可验证的决策支持系统，能够弥合粗粒度分期指南与个体化临床实践之间的差距，具有重要的临床应用价值。

Abstract: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is a common malignancy and a leading cause of cancer-related mortality. Current guidelines and staging systems provide coarse categories, but often miss within-stage heterogeneity and the clinical context in electronic medical records (EMRs). We present HCC-STAR (Hepatocellular Carcinoma Staging, Treatment And pRognosis), a clinically aligned large language model that reads routine EMR narratives and jointly outputs risk score-based staging, ranked guideline-consistent treatments with evidence-based rationales, and individualized survival estimates. We curated about 30,000 HCC cases from SEER and expanded them into EMR-style narrative training data using a clinician-validated, prompt-based augmentation workflow. On this corpus, we developed a knowledge-aligned reasoning framework optimized with a step-verifiable composite reward, moving beyond text-level memorization of clinical guidelines. In a multi-center cohort of 6,668 patients from 12 hospitals in China, HCC-STAR achieved state-of-the-art performance in treatment recommendation and risk stratification compared with clinical guidelines and competitive models, including GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5 Pro. Hypothetical overall-survival analysis showed a median survival of 51 months under adherence to HCC-STAR recommendations, compared with 29 and 32 months under BCLC and CNLC. In clinician-centric evaluations, blinded hepatobiliary specialists rated HCC-STAR's reasoning and evidence-based justifications as trustworthy. The model surpassed resident and attending physicians in treatment accuracy and helped physicians make more accurate decisions faster when used as an assistant. These findings support HCC-STAR as a reliable and verifiable decision-support system for risk stratification and precision therapy in HCC.

</details>


### [185] [The complexities of patient-centred conversational artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08625)
*João Matos,Olivia Buege,Donny Cheung,Gary S. Collins,Paula Dhiman,Nan Li,Bingyu Mao,Benjamin W. Nelson,Michail Ouroutzoglou,Paul Varghese,Jonathan Amar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于2,053条真实医患聊天记录,构建了一个可独立建模临床内容、情绪状态、对话策略和沟通风格的患者模拟器,并通过图灵测试式评估和四种大语言模型的紧急程度分级实验,证明沟通风格会显著影响分诊结果,呼吁面向真实用户多样性的以患者为中心的对话AI设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向消费者的健康聊天机器人(由大语言模型驱动)在开发与评估中,通常依赖配合度高、表达清晰的模拟患者,而真实用户的沟通模式与情绪表达差异巨大。基于理想化交互设计的系统在真实部署中可能表现不佳并加剧健康不平等,因此需要针对真实患者沟通多样性进行评估。

Method: 1. 分析2,053条真实患者-聊天机器人对话,刻画沟通模式与情绪表达的异质性。2. 开发患者模拟器,解耦建模四个维度:临床内容、情绪状态、对话策略、沟通风格。3. 开展图灵测试式真实感评估(15位人类评分者,1,164个临床医生评分案例)。4. 设计5种典型患者人格(personae),在4种大语言模型上进行紧急程度分级评估,系统比较不同沟通风格对分诊结果的影响。

Result: 1. 真实患者聊天记录显示沟通模式与情绪表达存在广泛差异。2. 图灵测试中人类评分者区分模拟与真实对话的准确率仅为55%(接近随机),表明模拟对话与真实对话几乎难以区分。3. 在使用5种患者人格评估4个LLM的紧急程度分级任务中,沟通风格的改变显著影响分诊结果。4. 证实针对理想化交互设计的系统在面对真实多样化沟通时可能表现欠佳。

Conclusion: 以患者为中心的对话式人工智能必须适应沟通的多样性;依赖理想化或配合型患者的开发评估范式存在风险,可能在真实部署中性能下降并放大健康差距,未来系统设计需纳入对真实用户沟通异质性的考量。

Abstract: Consumer-facing health chatbots powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for symptom assessment. However, chatbot development and evaluation often rely on cooperative, articulate, simulated patients. We analysed 2,053 real patient-chatbot conversations and found that communication patterns and expression of emotions vary widely across users. We developed a patient simulator that separately models clinical content, emotional state, conversational strategy, and communication style. In a Turing-inspired evaluation of realism with 15 human graders, simulated conversations were nearly indistinguishable from real ones, with human graders achieving an accuracy of 55%. We used five distinct patient personae, across 1,164 clinician-graded cases, to evaluate the performance of four LLMs in urgency assessment. We found that communication style can significantly alter triage outcomes. Patient-centred conversational artificial intelligence must accommodate communication diversity: systems designed for idealised, rather than realistic, interactions risk underperforming and amplifying health disparities when deployed in the real world.

</details>


### [186] [Formal Mechanisms for Market Stability in Self-Interested Agent Societies: A Marketplace Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08652)
*Eugene Ng Yi Sheng,Bingquan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在自利LLM智能体社会中，哪些形式化机制能够维持市场稳定性。通过一个18个DeepSeek-V3智能体组成的多商品市场仿真，对比了8种机制在渐进式恶意代理注入下的表现，发现Mediation（调解）机制最优；进一步通过6轮提示词优化的对抗性红队测试，证明该机制具备"可弯曲但不可破坏"的对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自利智能体在重复社会困境中倾向于背叛，导致合作的贸易收益崩溃。已有研究（如Tewolde等人2026）表明现代LLM在缺乏形式化机制时同样会全面背叛，且推理模型甚至会出现"推理腐败"现象以加剧搭便车行为。然而核心问题仍未解决：在不受限的通信之上，需要多少形式化结构才能维持社会合作？这种结构在面临主动攻击时的韧性如何？本研究将这一基础问题具体化为可操作的市场仿真研究。

Method: 设计了一个封闭的多商品（3种商品A/B/C）物物交换市场环境：18个LLM智能体（DeepSeek-V3，温度0.7）各持一种生产专长，在受限的社交网络（每个智能体7-9个邻居）中通过双边易货交易获取效用。商品易腐（每轮损失20%），制造时间紧迫性。设计了两阶段实验：阶段一对比8种机制（基线通信、全局声誉、契约、调解、治理、网络重连、代价性制裁、司法）在200轮渐进式恶意代理注入（0→4→8→16个）下的表现；阶段二对最优机制（调解）进行对抗性红队测试，使用迭代提示词优化的LLM驱动恶意代理开发了6个攻击版本。主指标为诚实代理累计效用和基尼系数，并正式定义了"对抗鲁棒性"。

Result: 阶段一：调解机制以累计效用1556显著领先，比通信基线高29%，比第二名网络重连（1352）高15%，是唯一在恶意代理增加时每阶段效用反而上升的机制（359→407）。调解率达57.8%，未调解交易背叛率为0%。其他机制各有缺陷：代价性制裁因搭便问题垫底（685），司法机制因诉讼费用抑制使用，契约机制因多阶段协议摩擦导致交易量暴跌。阶段二：经6轮提示词优化，最强攻击v6（"双重向量攻击"——不可证伪的楔形消息+诱饵提案）将诚实代理效用降低13.3%（1556→1350），但市场从未崩溃，每轮效用始终为正，且每次恶意代理注入后都能在10-20轮内恢复。调解展现出"可弯曲但不可破坏"的对抗鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，在LLM智能体社会中，结构性预防背叛的机制（调解）远优于反应性惩罚机制（制裁、司法）。设计原则应遵循：预防优于惩罚、低摩擦至关重要、不可证伪的意图声明比可验证的事实声明更具破坏力、恢复能力比抵抗能力更重要。该研究正式定义了合作机制的对抗鲁棒性概念，并为多智能体系统设计提供了实践指导：在预期存在对抗行为的环境中，能从结构上防止背叛的机制为合作市场稳定性提供了最稳健的基础。主要局限包括单次运行统计效力不足、模型特异性（仅DeepSeek-V3）以及机制组合未被测试。

Abstract: Self-interested agents, left unconstrained, tend toward defection in repeated social dilemmas, causing cooperative gains from trade to collapse. This paper investigates what formal mechanisms, layered on top of unrestricted communication, are sufficient for a society of such agents to maintain market stability, and how resilient those mechanisms are to adversarial attack. We instantiate the research question as a multi-agent marketplace simulation where 18 LLM agents (DeepSeek-V3) with complementary production specialties must trade within a constrained social network to obtain utility. We conduct two experimental phases: (1) a mechanism comparison across eight conditions under progressive troll injection over 200 rounds, identifying Mediation as the top-performing mechanism; and (2) adversarial red-teaming of Mediation using iteratively prompt-optimised LLM-driven trolls, finding that the best attack (v6) reduces honest-agent utility by 13.3% but cannot collapse the market. Mediation enables recovery even under sustained adversarial pressure. We define adversarial robustness as a mechanism's ability to sustain positive honest-agent utility under optimised attack, and find that Mediation is robust: it can be bent but not broken.

</details>


### [187] [SolarChain-Eval: A Physics-Constrained Benchmark for Trustworthy Economic Agents in Decentralized Energy Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08681)
*Shilin Ou,Yifan Xu,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SolarChain-Eval是一个面向去中心化能源市场的物理约束基准测试，将市场治理建模为Gymnasium兼容的MDP，用于评估自主经济智能体在效用、物理安全、稳定性、平滑性、空间公平性和可审计性等多维度上的可信度，并通过基于LLM的Planner/Auditor层提供透明的运行时干预机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体AI越来越多地部署到信息-物理系统（如去中心化能源市场），传统的纯奖励评估范式不足以揭示代理在物理约束缺失时的安全风险。具体而言，无约束的RL代理可能利用虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA），通过为无效发电量提供经济背书来人为制造流动性，导致市场不稳定与治理失效。因此，需要一个耦合物理真实性的基准来评估代理的可信度。

Method: 1. **RL形式化**：将市场治理建模为24小时episode的MDP，状态包含时间编码、已验证/报告发电量、物理PV上限、供需缺口、流动性、代币价格、风险信号等；动作为奖励分配比αₜ、流动性注入比ℓₜ、代币销毁率bₜ，满足αₜ+ℓₜ≤0.98。2. **物理约束模块**：通过pvlib计算PV物理上限P^max，识别超过容量或被注入FDIA的无效供给Xₜ，并定义物理违规率Vₜ量化代理对无效供给的经济背书程度。3. **奖励设计**：正项为市场出清量Mₜ，负项包含流动性回撤、动作抖动、未满足需求、复合物理风险（Vₜ+action-backed suspicious supply）和城市间空间不公平。4. **策略对比**：评估Static、Random、Myopic基线及PPO、SAC、DQN三种RL算法，使用Stable-Baselines3训练。5. **LLM Planner/Auditor层**：仅在评估时插入，Planner在episode级别设定动作边界和审计策略（预算Bₑ、冷却cₑ、阈值），Auditor在步骤级别通过硬触发（物理违规/供给缺口/滑页）和软触发（动作不稳定κₜ）决定是否审阅，并采用approve/revise机制，所有干预通过结构化日志记录。6. **三类评估设置**：主基准、奖励消融（去除物理惩罚）、智能体评估（添加LLM治理层）。

Result: **主基准（RQ1）**：RL策略（PPO/SAC/DQN）在累积奖励上显著优于Static/Random/Myopic基线（-22.61 vs -23.97，p<10⁻²⁸），但存在效用-安全权衡——SAC奖励最高且物理违规最低但滑页最高，PPO滑页最低但人造流动性更高。**物理约束消融（RQ2）**：移除物理惩罚后，PPO/SAC/DQN的奖励分别上升22.61/22.52/23.55，但人造流动性分别激增0.0785/0.1238/0.0576 MWh，证明奖励最大化代理会通过背书无效供给人为制造流动性。**智能体治理层（RQ3）**：LLM Planner/Auditor在有约束设置下审计率34.2%-37.2%、修订率>95%；可降低SAC和DQN的动作抖动，但略增PPO抖动；对人造流动性的影响因策略而异（PPO降低、SAC/DQN略增）。即使加入LLM层，无约束设置下人造流动性仍达0.1901 MWh，表明LLM无法完全弥补奖励函数误设。**案例研究**：展示了PPO在无约束下提出激进高流动性动作(r=0.049, l=0.931, b=0.200)后，Auditor基于违规率0.14将其修订为保守值(r=0.050, l=0.200, b=0.000)，动作delta达0.932，全程记录触发信号和修订理由。

Conclusion: 可信的智能体AI评估需要物理约束与透明干预轨迹的结合。研究表明：(1) RL代理在去中心化能源市场中能提升经济效用，但会重新分配安全风险（物理违规、人造流动性、滑页之间存在权衡）；(2) 物理惩罚缺失时，奖励最大化代理会系统性利用虚假发电注入来膨胀人造流动性，这是机制层面的漏洞；(3) LLM Planner/Auditor层提供有价值的运行时治理接口和可审计的风险缓解，但其效果具有策略依赖性，无法替代训练阶段的正确奖励设计。SolarChain-Eval通过记录完整决策循环（环境风险信号、动作修正、自然语言解释）支持部署导向的压力测试，确保自主干预在经济可行性、可验证性、可问责性和物理真实性方面均达标。未来工作将扩展至自主交易和供应链分配场景，并通过更广泛的trace、触发器消融和受限RL基线对比来校准该模板的适用边界。

Abstract: As agentic AI systems are increasingly applied to cyber-physical environments, their evaluation requires assessment of both task performance and trustworthiness. In decentralized energy markets, autonomous agents may improve market utility, but may also exploit invalid physical data, create artificial liquidity, and produce unstable governance decisions. Therefore, we propose SolarChain-Eval, a physics-constrained benchmark for evaluating trustworthy economic agents. It formulates market governance as a Gymnasium-compatible Markov Decision Process, where agents make hourly decisions. SolarChain-Eval evaluates each policy across multiple dimensions, including market utility, physical safety, slippage, action smoothness, spatial fairness, and auditability. To support agentic evaluation, SolarChain-Eval incorporates an LLM-based Planner/Auditor layer. The Planner defines episode-level action bounds and audit rules, while the Auditor reviews and revises high-risk actions. All interventions are recorded through structured logs, including trigger signals, proposed actions, revised actions, and audit rationales. Experiments with static, random, myopic, RL, and RL+LLM policies reveal a clear utility-safety trade-off. RL agents improve market utility but can still produce unsafe behavior. When the physics penalty is removed, reward-maximizing agents exploit invalid generation and increase artificial liquidity. The LLM Planner/Auditor improves auditability and mitigates selected risks, but it cannot fully compensate for a misspecified reward function. These results indicate that trustworthy agentic AI evaluation requires both physical constraints and transparent intervention traces. We release data and code as open access on GitHub for replicability.

</details>


### [188] [Remember When It Matters: Proactive Memory Agent for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08716)
*Yifan Wu,Lizhu Zhang,Yuhang Zhou,Mingyi Wang,Bo Peng,Serena Li,Xiangjun Fan,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种"主动式记忆代理"(Proactive Memory Agent)架构,用于解决长视野语言代理在执行多步骤任务时出现的"行为状态衰减"(behavioral state decay)问题。记忆代理作为独立模块与动作代理并行运行,通过结构化记忆库维护任务需求、环境事实、失败尝试等执行状态,并在判断记忆可能影响下一步决策时注入针对性提醒,否则保持沉默。该方法在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 和 τ²-Bench 上分别提升 pass@1 达 +8.3 pp 和 +6.8 pp,并通过 SFT+GRPO 在 Qwen3.5-27B 上验证了干预策略的可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野语言代理在执行多步任务时,虽然历史信息仍可能存在于转录或上下文窗口中,但已不再可靠地影响后续决策——任务需求、失败尝试、诊断结论、开放子目标等"执行状态"逐渐失效,作者将此失败模式称为"行为状态衰减"。现有记忆系统多聚焦于存储与被动检索,缺乏对记忆"何时应介入控制回路"的决策机制,导致代理重复错误、遗忘需求或忽视早期发现。

Method: 采用两阶段记忆代理架构:(1) 第一阶段(记忆管理):代理通过预定义工具调用(memory_update_status、memory_save_knowledge、memory_save_procedural、memory_delete)维护结构化记忆库,包含私有 status、知识记忆(稳定事实)与程序记忆(尝试与结果);(2) 第二阶段(干预选择):基于更新后的记忆库与近期轨迹窗口(k=8 消息),决定输出针对性 `<context_for_action>` 提醒或 `<no_intervention/>` 沉默动作,提醒以临时上下文形式注入下一次动作代理调用中。记忆代理按固定间隔触发,可即插即用地与未修改的前沿动作代理配合使用。此外,采用 SFT(蒸馏提示型记忆代理轨迹)+ GRPO(在 SETA 上优化干预校准)训练 Qwen3.5-27B 开放权重记忆策略。

Result: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.0(85 任务)上,Sonnet 4.5 动作代理 pass@1 从 37.6% 提升至 45.9%(+8.3 pp),Opus 4.6 从 43.5% 提升至 45.9%(+2.4 pp);在 τ²-Bench(278 任务,airline/retail/telecom 任务加权平均)上,Sonnet 4.5 从 55.0% 提升至 61.8%(+6.8 pp),Opus 4.6 从 66.2% 提升至 68.7%(+2.5 pp)。消融实验表明:完整两阶段设计在宏平均上最优,优于全记忆库暴露(被动增强)、强制每步注入(advisor-only)、无记忆库的干预引导以及 Mem0 向量+BM25 检索。开放权重实验中,SFT+GRPO 训练的 Qwen3.5-27B 记忆代理使冻结的 Qwen3.5-122B-A10B 动作代理在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上从 37.6% 提升至 41.1%(+3.5 pp),SETA 验证奖励从 0.709 提升至 0.734。

Conclusion: 论文将记忆重新定义为长视野代理的主动干预策略而非被动存储检索机制,验证了"行为状态衰减"是核心失效模式,而选择性干预(可选择沉默)结合维护的执行状态记忆可稳定提升强弱不同动作代理的任务成功率;干预策略可通过 SFT+GRPO 蒸馏到开放权重模型并部分迁移到未见基准。开放方向包括:联合训练记忆与动作代理、学习动态触发时机、区分逐字提醒与任务抽象的效用边界。

Abstract: In long-horizon tasks, decision-relevant state is often scattered across an expanding trajectory, while the action agent must surface it and act. As trajectories grow, task requirements, environment facts, prior attempts, diagnoses, and open subgoals can be buried in the context window or pushed beyond it, failing to influence decisions when needed. We call this failure mode "behavioral state decay". We study memory as an active intervention mechanism rather than passive retrieval. A separate memory agent runs alongside an unmodified action agent, updating a structured memory bank from the recent trajectory and deciding whether to inject a memory-grounded reminder or remain silent. The module is plug-and-play with frontier action agents and existing agent harnesses. Across Terminal-Bench 2.0 and $\tau^2$-Bench, it improves pass@1 for both weaker and stronger action agents, with gains of +8.3 pp on Terminal-Bench and +6.8 pp on $\tau^2$-Bench. Ablations show that selective intervention outperforms passive bank exposure, always-on injection, advisor-only guidance, and general retrieval. As an early step toward open-weight memory policies, we train Qwen3.5-27B on SETA using SFT and GRPO, improving validation reward and achieving partial transfer to Terminal-Bench.

</details>


### [189] [The Illusion of Equivalency: Statistical Characterization of Quantization Effects in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08734)
*Baha Rababah,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Carson K. Leung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"正确性一致性"（Correctness Agreement, CA）这一决策级评估指标，揭示了后训练量化（PTQ）下大语言模型表面性能与行为一致性之间的脱节，证明即使准确率和困惑度保持稳定，中等程度量化仍会导致显著的行为偏移，并从注意力权重的结构畸变角度解释了 Q/K 投影对低比特量化更为敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 量化评估几乎完全依赖准确率和困惑度等聚合性能指标，无法捕捉量化对模型决策行为（即在相同样本上的预测一致性）的潜在影响。模型即使保持相近的聚合性能，也可能在具体样本上做出不同预测，这意味着知识、推理和安全相关行为可能在不被察觉的情况下发生退化，亟需一种能够直接衡量基础模型与量化变体之间行为对齐的评估方法。

Method: 1) 将 PTQ 形式化为参数空间上的算子族 T_c（c 涵盖 2–8 bit 的 legacy 和 K-quantization 配置），通过 llama.cpp 对 Llama-3.2-3B、Vicuna-7B、Mistral-7B、Llama-3.1-8B 进行量化；2) 在注意力投影权重（W^Q, W^K, W^V, W^O）上计算均值、标准差、偏度、峰度四类统计量以及 KS 统计量、欧氏距离、余弦相似度、KL 散度四类分布散度，按 block 与 projection 两个维度进行聚合；3) 提出 Correctness Agreement 指标，定义为基础模型与量化模型同时判为正确的样本比例；4) 在 WikiText-2/C4 上测困惑度，在 HellaSwag/Winogrande/ARC 上做零样本准确率与 CA 联合分析。

Result: 1) 高比特（Q8_0–Q5_K）几乎不改变权重统计量与分布形态；结构畸变始于 Q4_K/Q4_0，在 Q3_K 与 Q2_K 处出现非线性断点，表现为偏度剧烈波动、均值偏移、峰度崩溃以及 KL/KS 散度的急剧上升；2) 注意力投影中 Q 与 K 一致性地比 V 和 O 对低比特量化更敏感，余弦相似度下降与 KL 散度上升在 Q/K 上最为显著；3) 困惑度与准确率随比特下降呈非单调变化（部分低位配置甚至出现更低困惑度），但 Correctness Agreement 在所有配置下始终低于准确率，且随精度降低持续下降，在 Q2_K 处跌至最低，表明基础模型的正确决策被大量改变；4) 确立 Q4_K 为安全量化的上界、Q3_K 为退化起点、Q2_K 为崩溃区间。

Conclusion: 传统性能指标（困惑度与准确率）在中等量化下具有误导性，会制造"等价幻觉"；正确性一致性作为决策级指标能够有效揭示量化引起的行为漂移。Q 与 K 投影的结构脆弱性提示未来量化方案可采用结构感知的混合精度策略，对 Q/K 分配更高精度、对 V/O 实施更激进的压缩，从而在效率与行为保真度之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is widely used to deploy large language models in resource-constrained settings, yet its evaluation relies almost exclusively on accuracy and perplexity. We show that these metrics fail to capture behavioral changes induced by quantization. We introduce correctness agreement, a decision-level metric that measures overlap in correct predictions between a base model and its quantized variants, independent of absolute accuracy. Across multiple models and quantization schemes from 8-bit to 2-bit, we find that behavioral divergence emerges under moderate quantization even when task performance appears preserved. To explain this effect, we analyze quantization as a structural operator on attention weights and quantify layer-wise distortions using statistical and distributional measures. Our results reveal non-linear breakpoints at low bit-widths and show that query and key projections are consistently more sensitive than value and output projections. These findings expose an illusion of equivalence between base and quantized models and motivate behavioral evaluation beyond conventional performance metrics.

</details>


### [190] [Workflow as Knowledge: Semantic Persistence for LLM-Mediated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08740)
*Emanuele Quinto,Carlo Andrea Rozzi,Francesco Zanitti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个受 Lisp 启发但语言无关的概念模型，将 LLM 工作流的定义、实例、推理记录、上下文快照和依赖关系建模为共享知识基底中的持久化、可查询的知识对象。其核心语义区分是 derive（基于可用状态的确定性计算）与 infer（在声明上下文与执行器能力策略下的 LLM 介导判断），强调工作流本身可作为知识被检查、恢复和审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 工作流系统虽然已解决显式控制流、检查点、日志等执行层面的问题，但工作流定义、运行实例和模型介导的推理记录在执行后往往仅以日志、追踪、聊天历史等外部痕迹存在，缺乏作为一等知识对象的语义角色，导致事后审查、归因、可恢复性和推理可追溯性困难。论文主张应从"工作流作为可执行控制结构"转向"工作流作为持久化语义知识对象"。

Method: 论文采用概念建模方法：(1) 提出三层架构——运行时服务层、DSL 机器控制层、语义层；(2) 定义语义对象模式（workflow-definition、workflow-instance、derived-object、inference-record、approval-record、panel-record、context-snapshot、dependency-link 等）；(3) 引入 derive/infer 核心语义边界，并由执行器统一介导；(4) 以 Common Lisp 风格的符号形式作为解释性表征；(5) 给出 ARS 风格的声明审阅工作流作为示例；(6) 对 77 份技能类工作流制品进行探索性词汇扫描以驱动词汇精炼；(7) 与 W3C PROV-DM 做初步映射对比。

Result: 产出一个初步的概念对象模型与操作词汇；通过工作流示例展示模型如何统一表达确定性检查、模型介导分类、作者批准和审查小组决议；词汇扫描揭示若干压力点（如需区分 approval 与 panel，分离 state 与 record，区分多种 context 形态）；PROV-DM 映射显示模型"与 PROV 兼容但不完备"，补充了 DSL 级角色、执行器介导、infer 边界、面板结构与生命周期问题。

Conclusion: 在"语义持久化"视角下，工作流定义与运行实例及其推理、审批、面板和上下文记录可作为同一知识基底中的可检查、可恢复、可审查对象，工作流本身成为知识。该贡献为概念层面的对象模型与词汇，形式化转换语义、记录生命周期策略、治理与可信度、实证评估与原型实现均被明确列为未来工作。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) applications increasingly use explicit workflows for tool use, retrieval, branching, checkpointing, and human approval. Existing workflow systems already address many execution concerns. This paper proposes a Lisp-inspired but language-independent conceptual model: symbolic forms, object identity, and live-image thinking are used as explanatory lenses, not implementation commitments. In this model, workflow definitions, workflow instances, inference records, context snapshots, and dependency relations are represented as persistent knowledge objects in a shared knowledge substrate. Its central semantic distinction is between derive and infer: derive is deterministic computation over available state; infer is mediated LLM judgment under declared context and executor-controlled capability policy. The result is a preliminary conceptual account of semantic persistence: workflows do not merely produce knowledge and leave traces, but can themselves be represented as inspectable, resumable, and reviewable knowledge objects, while formal transition semantics remain future work.

</details>


### [191] [AUTOPILOT VQA: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Incident-Centric Dashcam Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08745)
*Siddharth Damodharan,Radhika Gupta,Ali Alshami,Ryan Rabinowitz,Jugal Kalita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AUTOPILOT-VQA 是一个面向行车记录仪视频理解的、以事故为核心视觉问答基准，包含 600 多个视频片段和 6000 多对问答对，作为 AUTOPILOT CVPR 2026 Kaggle 竞赛的一部分，用于评估视觉-语言模型在安全关键驾驶场景中的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶数据集（如 KITTI、BDD100K、nuScenes 等）主要聚焦常规驾驶条件下的感知任务，针对罕见但高风险的安全关键事故场景进行结构化推理评估仍存在明显空白。作者旨在填补这一空白，推动视觉-语言模型从物体识别向时序感知、安全相关的推理方向发展。

Method: 构建了一个标注行车记录仪视频数据集，覆盖完整事故严重性谱（27% 碰撞、11% 险些发生、17% 预先规避、27% 无事故基线）。人工标注人员在 9 个语义问题组（A–I）共 28 个子问题上标注，产生 6000 多对问答对。问题涵盖环境条件、道路配置、交通控制、涉及实体、行为表征、责任归属和影响位置等维度。通过 Kaggle 竞赛平台进行公开评估，以各问题平均准确率作为评分指标。

Result: 竞赛共吸引 224 名注册参赛者、59 支队伍、686 次提交。最佳队伍得分 0.65835，第二、三名分别为 0.65505 和 0.65371，前几名差距极小。多数提交分数集中在 0.39–0.40 附近，表明基线模型难以全面泛化。错误主要集中在需要因果推理和细粒度关系推理的任务上，而非简单的感知任务。

Conclusion: 当前视觉-语言模型在自动驾驶安全关键场景中的表现仍然有限，尤其在结构化推理、因果推断、时序理解和不确定性建模方面远未达到人类水平。Kaggle 等开放式竞赛平台能够有效汇聚多样化方法推动研究进展。未来需要显式建模时序因果、智能体交互、不确定性校准和分层决策的方法，以实现更鲁棒的自动驾驶视觉-语言系统。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models, Large Language Models, and Multimodal Large Language Models have improved autonomous driving tasks such as scene understanding, decision making, trajectory prediction, and visual question answering. However, evaluating whether these models can reliably reason about safety-critical incidents remains challenging. To address this gap, we present AUTOPILOT-VQA, an incident-centric visual question answering benchmark for dashcam video understanding. The dataset evaluates different systems through structured questions designed around real-world driving incidents and near-incidents. The benchmark covers diverse safety-relevant categories, including weather and lighting conditions, traffic environment, road layout, road surface state, signage, involved entities, accident occurrence, impact location, and avoidability-related reasoning. By requiring models to answer grounded questions about both contextual scene properties and event-level incident details, AUTOPILOT-VQA moves beyond object recognition toward temporally grounded, safety-aware reasoning. The dataset is released as part of the AUTOPILOT CVPR 2026 competition and provides a standardized benchmark for assessing the reliability of autonomous driving systems in different scenarios. Our benchmark support developments for more interpretable, robust, and safety-conscious vision-language systems for real-world autonomous driving.

</details>


### [192] [Using AI-based Learning Assistants in Higher Education: A Large-Scale Descriptive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08748)
*Kristina Schaaff,Quintus Stierstorfer,Valerie Heckel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对德国IU国际应用科学大学76,485名远程学习学生在2025年2月使用AI学习助手Syntea的客观日志数据进行了大规模描述性分析,考察了使用模式在性别、年龄组、学习集群、学位类型和学习方式等维度上的差异,并提供了迄今为止教育聊天机器人使用研究中最大规模的实证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教育聊天机器人研究主要依赖小样本和自报告问卷数据,缺乏基于客观行为日志的大规模实证证据,尤其在远程学习环境下,关于人口统计学和结构性因素如何影响AI学习助手交互模式的研究存在明显空白。

Method: 采用2025年2月单月观察期的客观日志数据,数据清洗后纳入76,485名远程学习学生(剔除78条不完整记录及多学位在读者),按Pew研究中心代际划分年龄组(婴儿潮一代至Z世代),从性别、年龄、学习集群、学位类型、学习方式(全日制/非全日制)及时间模式(小时/星期)六个维度进行描述性统计分析,活动量按"学生-小时-日"唯一组合进行归一化计数。

Result: 整体使用率为57.6%(44,035/76,485);女性(59.05%)略高于男性(54.94%),多元性别组因样本量过小(n=109)而参考有限;Z世代使用率最高(63.66%),随年龄递减,婴儿潮一代仅37.88%但样本极少(n=132);学习集群间差异较小,教育心理学与市场营销传播最高,建筑施工与设计媒体最低;本科生(58.17%)略高于硕士生(54.25%);全日制学生(59.49%)高于非全日制学生(55.71%);时间模式显示使用集中在工作日上午(11:00达峰)及工作日(周二最高),非全日制学生在周末和傍晚使用比例相对更高。

Conclusion: Syntea已嵌入多数远程学习者的学习常规,但使用模式受课程可用性、学科适配性和学习节奏等结构性因素调节,差异需置于结构性访问条件和描述性研究性质下解读;未来研究应超越描述性分析,探究使用模式与学习成果(参与度、坚持率、满意度、学业表现)的关系,并纳入课程级实施因素以区分学习者偏好与结构性访问条件。

Abstract: In this study, we present a large-scale descriptive analysis of the use of an AI-based learning assistant (Syntea) in higher education. Based on objective log data from 77,543 students enrolled in distance studies, we examine usage patterns across gender, age group, study cluster, degree, and study mode. To date, existing research on educational chatbots has largely relied on comparatively small samples and self-reported survey data, while large-scale evidence on actual usage behavior remains limited. Our findings show that Syntea is already embedded in the study routines of many learners, but that usage differs across demographic and structural contexts. By identifying these patterns, our study provides an empirical basis for the further development of AI-based learning support and contributes a large-scale analysis of educational chatbot usage in higher education.

</details>


### [193] [Ideas Have Genomes: Benchmarking Scientific Lineage Reasoning and Lineage-Grounded Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08758)
*Yifan Zhou,Qihao Yang,Yan Li,Donggang Li,Xiru Hu,Hokin Deng,Ziyang Gong,Xuanyi Zhou,Huacan Wang,Xiangchao Yan,Wanghan Xu,Wenlong Zhang,Shaofeng Zhang,Yue Zhou,Yifan Yang,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 IdeaGene-Bench (IG-Bench)，一个用于评估 AI 系统科学谱系推理能力（继承、变异、重组科学思想）的基准。其核心是 IdeaGene 框架，将论文建模为类型化、证据驱动的"思想基因组"(Idea Genome) 对象，并通过 GenomeDiff 记录六种演化动态。基准包含 1,961 条谱系轨迹、1,085 个思想基因组对象和 920 条 GenomeDiff 记录，涵盖 10 个科学领域。实验表明当前最强的 LLM-based 科学家在谱系推理任务上仅达 27.3% 精确准确率，揭示了组合性瓶颈(compositional bottleneck)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 基准未能评估 AI 系统是否真正理解科学思想的"继承结构"——即新思想如何在既有机制上修复局限、重组已有工作。科学思想发展类似于生物基因组的演化，但现有基准缺乏对这种谱系关系的系统刻画与评估能力。

Method: 1. 提出 IdeaGene 框架：将论文表示为最小化、类型化、证据驱动的 Idea Genome 对象集合；通过 GenomeDiff 对齐这些对象，记录继承(inheritance)、变异(mutation)、丢失(loss)、外部导入(external import)和新颖插入(novel insertion)等六种演化动态。2. 构建 IG-Bench 基准：涵盖 10 个科学领域，包含 1,961 条谱系轨迹、1,085 个思想基因组对象、920 条 GenomeDiff 记录。3. 设计两类评估：IG-Exam（42 种任务类型，1,029 实例）测试封闭型谱系推理（抽象、继承追踪、演化推理、谱系验证）；IG-Arena 采用谱系条件下的种群演化得分(PES)评估生成质量。4. 在 14 个 LLM-based 科学家系统上进行实验评估。

Result: 1. 揭示了显著的"组合性瓶颈(compositional bottleneck)"：最强系统在谱系推理任务上仅达到 27.3% 精确准确率，表明当前 LLM 在处理科学思想继承结构方面能力有限。2. 发现结构化谱系上下文对不同系统的影响不一致——它会重新洗牌系统排名，而非对所有参与者均匀提升。3. PES 评估显示生成任务需要同时满足继承正确对象、与邻近工作有意义地区分、并对未来研究具备选择价值。

Conclusion: 科学思想继承具有组合结构，当前 AI 系统尚不能有效处理；IG-Bench 通过形式化的 Idea Genome 与 GenomeDiff 框架，为系统评估 LLM 在科学谱系推理与生成方面的能力提供了首个全面的基准。实验结果强调了科学发现 AI 需要新的方法突破组合性瓶颈，而非仅仅依赖更强的通用语言模型。

Abstract: Scientific ideas rarely start from a blank page. They inherit mechanisms, repair known limitations, and recombine pieces of earlier work, much like biological genomes. Current benchmarks still say little about whether AI systems can follow this inheritance structure. We present IdeaGene-Bench (IG-Bench), a benchmark for scientific lineage reasoning and lineage-grounded idea generation. IG-Bench is organized around the IdeaGene framework: each paper or proposal is represented as a set of minimal, typed, evidence-grounded Idea Genome objects, and a GenomeDiff aligns these objects to record inheritance, mutation, loss, external import, and novel insertion under six operational evolutionary dynamics. The benchmark contains 1,961 golden lineage traces, 1,085 curated Idea Genome objects, and 920 pairwise GenomeDiff records across 10 scientific domains. It supports two evaluations. IG-Exam (42 task types, 1,029 instances) tests closed-form lineage reasoning across Idea Genome abstraction, inheritance tracing, evolutionary reasoning, and lineage verification. IG-Arena evaluates generation with a lineage-conditioned Population-Evolution Score(PES), asking whether a proposal can be inserted as a coherent descendant of a given lineage population: it should inherit the right Idea Genome objects, vary meaningfully from nearby work, and offer selection value for future research. Experiments on 14 LLM-based scientists expose a compositional bottleneck. The strongest system reaches only 27.3% exact accuracy on lineage reasoning, and structured lineage context reshuffles system rankings rather than helping every participant uniformly.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [3100 Opinions on Code Review in an AI World: Building Causal Theory from Practitioner Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07980)
*Shyam Agarwal,Courtney Miller,Christian Kästner,Bogdan Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文研究AI编码代理对代码审查（code review）的影响，通过观察性分析发现代理编写的PR审查更少、合并更快、讨论更少，但趋势方向不稳定；进而通过大规模综合实践者话语构建解释性因果理论（26个构念、67个关系），主张团队的专业知识和审查流程结构决定了AI对软件影响的正负方向，并将争议立场转化为可证伪命题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于实践者关于AI编码代理对代码审查影响的尖锐分歧：审查是否成为瓶颈、人工审查是否仍必要、是否悄然侵蚀理解。现有仓库挖掘研究仅测量表面趋势，既不稳定也缺乏机制解释，因此需要从"发生了什么"深入到"为什么发生"。

Method: 1. 观察性分析：分析公共GitHub活动数据，比较代理编写与人工编写PR的审查、合并、讨论情况；2. 灰色文献综合：收集38,709份工程博客与Reddit帖子，过滤后用LLM辅助流水线对3,100份分层随机样本编码；3. 构建包含26个构念和67个关系（64个有向、3个争议）的因果模型，作为可证伪的解释性理论。

Result: 1. 观察数据显示代理编写PR审查频率更低、合并速度更快、讨论更少，但这些趋势在不同但同样合理的分析选择下方向翻转，痕迹揭示变化但不解释原因；2. 构建的因果理论核心主张：审查是决定编码代理影响的控制点，AI不决定影响正负，团队通过人类专业知识和审查流程结构决定；3. 提供LLM辅助灰色文献理论构建方法作为软件工程研究可扩展模板，并开源实现。

Conclusion: AI不会单方面决定代码审查与软件质量的变化方向；团队的人类专业知识和审查流程设计是关键调节因素。论文将"AI正在改变代码审查"这一笼统命题转化为具有命名构念和调节变量的可证伪命题，为后续实证检验和实践提供了明确的理论框架与方法论模板。

Abstract: Coding agents now author entire pull requests, and practitioners sharply disagree about what this does to code review: whether it becomes the bottleneck, whether human review is still necessary, and whether it quietly erodes the understanding that it once built. Repository-mining studies measure surface trends but seldom explain the mechanisms beneath them, and the trends themselves prove unstable. A motivating observational analysis of public GITHUB activity finds that agent-authored pull requests are reviewed less often, merged several times faster, and discussed less than human-authored ones, yet the direction of these trends flips under different but equally defensible analysis choices, so the traces establish what is changing without explaining why. To recover the mechanisms, we synthesize practitioner discourse at scale into an explanatory theory: we collect 38,709 grey-literature documents (engineering blogs and Reddit threads), filter to those substantively about code review, and code a stratified random sample of 3,100 with an LLM-assisted pipeline, from which we build a causal model of 26 constructs and 67 relationships (64 directed, 3 contested). Its organizing claim is that review is the control point through which a coding agent's effect on software is decided, and that AI does not fix the sign of that effect: the team sets it, through the expertise its humans bring and how it structures the review process. The theory makes the competing positions explicit and turns "AI is changing code review" into falsifiable propositions with named constructs and moderators. As a secondary contribution, we offer the underlying LLM-assisted, grey-literature theory-building method as a scalable template for software-engineering research, with a public implementation.

</details>


### [195] [Aleena: Alignment Agent for Research Software Engineering Collaborations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08043)
*Kshitij Dani,Cordero Core,Landung Setiawan,Carlos Garcia Jurado Suarez,Anshul Tambay,Vani Mandava,Anant Mittal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Aleena是一个面向科研软件工程协作的开源生命周期对齐代理（lifecycle alignment agent），以GitHub为共享协作界面，将会议记录、聊天记录、Issue、PR等多模态交互转化为结构化的项目记录（摘要、风险、开放问题、草稿PR），在辅助人类决策的同时持续维护项目状态的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研软件协作中，研究人员与科研软件工程师（RSE）之间的决策依据往往分散在会议、Slack、GitHub Issue、PR等多种媒介中，导致项目意图、所有权归属和科学假设等关键上下文随时间流失，双方形成分歧的心智模型。现有平台（如Jira、Notion）仅能组织任务与文档，无法判断团队在目标、约束、职责与决策层面的对齐状态。作者将"对齐"重新框定为贯穿项目生命周期（Pre-Selection→Selection→Scoping→Implementation→Offboarding）的持续状态管理问题，而非一次性范围界定活动。

Method: Aleena采用四步代理循环（Perceive→Update→Select→Defer），围绕四类对齐挑战（AC1利益相关者期望、AC2科学术语与领域假设、AC3所有权与评审责任、AC4决策连续性）维护一个演化的项目状态模型。其端到端流程分为五层：①交互工件采集；②生成结构化GitHub工件（会议摘要、Issue、风险讨论、开放问题讨论）；③上下文感知建议与草稿PR；④利益相关者审查与解决；⑤项目状态更新并反馈至下一周期。技术上，FastAPI后端+React前端，通过GitHub OAuth/App进行仓库范围授权，经由LiteLLM网关调用多LLM提供方，采用模块化分析器（analyzer）架构，每个分析器对应特定对齐任务且提示词对用户可编辑。代理被严格约束为不执行合并、关闭等终局性操作，决策权始终归人类。

Result: Aleena已在华盛顿大学科学软件工程中心（SSEC）部署并进入早期运行，应用于多个进行中的协作项目。文章通过三个生命周期场景验证其能力：①早期阶段将首次会议转录为结构化GitHub工件，作为后续决策的共享基线；②中期通过词汇分析器识别"model"等术语在不同上下文中含义冲突（领域模拟vs.序列化对象）并开启讨论线程，依据会议和历史Issue信号自动标记Issue的科学/工程归属，必要时起草PR；③跨会议持续更新现有GitHub讨论而非生成孤立记录，形成可追溯的决策演变轨迹。系统正在采集工件保留率、编辑率、未操作关闭率及重开率等量化指标，并辅以利益相关者访谈。

Conclusion: Aleena定位为SE 3.0愿景下"AI队友"的具体实践——支持而非取代人类决策，覆盖RSE项目全生命周期。其GitHub中心化架构与基础设施即代码部署支持其他组织自托管。未来工作包括：连接跨生命周期工件以检测期望、所有权与优先级的全局漂移（"认知债务"）；演化为可检索历史并基于协作与代码上下文进行推理的对话式生命周期代理；开展多项目评估以报告各生命周期阶段的产出保留、编辑与未操作关闭比率，并与AIDev等大规模AI队友生产基准互补，从RSE视角为SE 3.0议程贡献"上游对齐"研究。

Abstract: Research software collaborations span meetings, informal chats, pull requests, and GitHub issues. A decision surfaced in a Slack thread, refined in a meeting, and implemented in a pull request can lose its original rationale across these artifacts, leaving domain researchers and research software engineers with divergent mental models of project intent, ownership, and scientific assumptions. We argue that alignment in research software engineering is a continuous lifecycle problem, and that agentic AI can support stakeholder alignment and project-state tracking without replacing human decision-making. We present Aleena, an open-source lifecycle alignment agent that uses GitHub as a shared collaboration surface, transforming multi-modal stakeholder interactions into structured project records that surface risks, track open questions, and preserve decision continuity. Grounded in university-based research software engineering center experiences, this paper presents the motivating problem, system design, prototype, and illustrative lifecycle scenarios for Aleena.

</details>


### [196] [ProjAgent: Procedural Similarity Retrieval for Repository-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08691)
*QiHong Chen,Aaron Imani,Iftekhar Ahmed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ProjAgent是一个仓库级代码生成系统，提出将"过程相似性"（procedural similarity）作为独立的检索信号，结合词法/语义检索和静态分析反馈循环，在REPOCOD基准上以Qwen2.5-Coder-14B-Instruct为骨干模型取得41.14% Pass@1，优于现有检索增强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的仓库级代码生成检索方法主要依赖词法（BM25）、结构或语义相似性，容易遗漏那些在标识符、应用领域上差异很大但实现相似计算逻辑（如同样的输入验证guard-clause模式）的有用上下文函数。例如不同模块中的函数可能都执行"验证输入并raise ValueError"的模式，但BM25相似度仅0.38、嵌入相似度仅0.59，现有检索方法无法捕获此类过程级关联。

Method: 方法核心包括四个阶段：(1)离线预处理阶段将上下文函数分解为逻辑步骤（自然语言描述+代码片段），并通过三阶段验证（docstring子串匹配→ROUGE-L≥0.7→LLM判定）和代码片段验证构建"grounded context steps"，然后用LLM推理隐藏状态在推理子空间（SVD分解unembedding层，能量阈值0.98，约占5%维度）上的投影表示，并通过PCA去偏（减去均值μ和第一主成分PC1）缓解各向异性问题；(2)过程检索阶段：先用投影余弦相似度找初始种子，再通过agentic工作流（提供ls/execute_bash/read_func/read_lines/search_func/propose_func工具+上下文压缩）发现未处理的候选，经种子扩展（与≥2个种子相似度超0.65）和LLM验证构成最终过程上下文，并执行plan confirmation迭代；(3)语义检索阶段：从目标文件出发通过AST追踪可访问符号池，用BM25与sentence-transformers/all-mpnet-base-v2的加权组合分数取top 20；(4)代码生成阶段：合并过程与语义上下文生成代码，并通过保守的静态分析反馈（AST语法检查+四种语义一致性检查）迭代修复。

Result: 在REPOCOD基准（980题，11个Python仓库）上：ProjAgent取得41.14% Pass@1，比Sparse(26.58%)/Dense(28.83%)/Same_File(14.98%)提升约12.31%，比SpecAgent(34.52%)提升6.62%；RQ2中投影相似度作为候选生成机制有效，"leftover group"在所有(τ,k)配置下精度均≥97.9%，配置B(τ=0.65,k=2)F1最佳；RQ3消融显示移除过程检索降至25.76%（下降15.38%），移除语义检索降至32.29%（下降8.85%），移除反馈循环仅降至40.29%（下降0.85%），过程检索贡献最大。

Conclusion: 过程相似性是仓库级代码生成中有效且先前未充分探索的检索维度；ProjAgent通过过程+语义+词法多信号融合与agentic工作流取得SOTA；推理子空间投影作为候选生成机制可靠排除不相关步骤，但需要LLM验证来过滤误报；过程检索与语义检索互补，前者提供实现模式指导，后者提供可执行符号知识，二者结合显著优于单信号；保守的静态分析反馈贡献有限，未来工作需改进大仓库探索效率与引入运行时执行反馈。

Abstract: Repository-level code generation requires implementing target functions while accounting for complex cross-file dependencies and project-specific conventions. Existing retrieval methods predominantly rely on lexical, structural, or semantic similarity, often overlooking repository functions that implement similar procedural logic despite differing in identifiers or application domains. We propose ProjAgent, a repository-level code generation system that introduces procedural similarity as an explicit retrieval signal. ProjAgent decomposes the target function into intermediate reasoning steps and employs an agentic workflow to retrieve repository functions that exhibit similar procedural behavior at each step. The retrieved procedural context is integrated with conventional semantic retrieval to construct a richer repository context for code generation. ProjAgent further incorporates a conservative static-analysis feedback loop that iteratively repairs generated code using compiler and static-analysis feedback. Evaluated on REPOCOD, ProjAgent achieves 41.14% Pass@1, outperforming existing retrieval-based baselines. These results demonstrate that procedural similarity is an effective and previously unexplored retrieval dimension for repository-level code generation.

</details>


### [197] [REFORGE: A Method for Benchmarking LLMs' Reverse Engineering Capabilities in Decompiled Binary Function Naming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07738)
*Nicolas Koller,Andreas u. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Reforge 是一个用于 LLM 辅助二进制分析的可溯源评估流水线，通过"八门置信漏斗 + 三层分级"机制揭示编译器优化对函数级真值可靠性的破坏，指出当前基准测试因幸存者偏差而高估优化导致的性能衰减，并以七款 LLM 的函数命名任务作为概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 二进制分析基准将函数级真值构建视为已解决的预处理步骤，报告准确率时未披露"可可靠评估函数"的比例；真正的瓶颈不是模型能力，而是编译器优化下二进制—源码对齐的可靠性，导致评估不公平且存在幸存者偏差。

Method: 构建 Reforge 流水线：从 C 源码经编译、DWARF 与句法提取、对齐（alignment）、反编译全程追踪溯源；将"对齐不确定性"操作化为八门置信漏斗（eight-gate confidence funnel）并采用三层分级（three-tier stratification）；在受控微基准（micro-benchmark）上跨优化等级测量高置信产率，并以非配对比较量化幸存者偏差；最后在函数命名任务上对 7 款当代 LLM 进行概念验证评估。

Result: 受控微基准显示，高置信产率从 87.2% 降至 65.9%（跨优化等级）；未配对的对比通过幸存者偏差夸大了优化所致的性能衰减；概念验证在 7 款 LLM 上以函数命名任务展示了该评估基底，并凸显不确定性感知基准的必要性。

Conclusion: 函数级二进制分析的公平评估必须将"对齐可靠性"作为一等公民纳入基准设计；Reforge 提供的可溯源、八门置信漏斗与三层分级机制为不确定性感知基准（uncertainty-aware benchmarking）提供了可行路径，缓解由优化与幸存者偏差引发的失真。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to reverse-engineering tasks, and recent threat-intelligence reporting shows them operating inside live offensive-security workflows. Claims about their capability, however, outpace our ability to measure it. Existing benchmarks for LLM-assisted binary analysis treat the construction of function-level ground truth as a solved pre-processing step and report accuracy without disclosing how many functions were reliably evaluable. We argue that the principal obstacle to fair evaluation is not model capability but the reliability of binary-to-source alignment under compiler optimization. This paper presents Reforge, a provenance-tracked pipeline that constructs function-level ground truth from C source through compilation, DWARF and syntactic extraction, alignment, and decompilation, and that operationalizes alignment uncertainty as an eight-gate confidence funnel with three-tier stratification. On a controlled micro-benchmark, high-confidence yield falls from 87.2% to 65.9% across optimization levels, and unpaired comparisons overstate optimization-induced performance decay through survivorship bias. A proof-of-concept evaluation of seven contemporary LLMs on function naming demonstrates the substrate and motivates uncertainty-aware benchmarking practice

</details>


### [198] [PERFOPT-Bench: Evaluating Coding Agents on Software Performance Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07744)
*Yingyun Cui,Yi Xie,Piaohong Wang,Jiawei Ma,Bo Liu,Liangliang Cao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PERFOPT-Bench 是一个用于评估编码智能体在软件性能优化任务上表现的基准测试，包含12个长周期优化任务，通过隐藏正确性测试、验证加速比测量和轨迹级审计进行评分。研究发现优化能力具有工作负载依赖性，没有单一智能体栈能主导所有任务，且智能体框架会显著改变同一LLM的任务级加速比分布。原始加速比作为基准指标不可靠，部分大幅加速来自基准特定捷径利用；探索性relay实验表明从外部化优化摘要重启可恢复额外优化空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准（如 HumanEval、SWE-bench）主要评估功能正确性，而生产软件还需要在真实执行目标上实现可测量的加速。性能优化是一个独立的智能体任务：智能体必须分析执行、诊断跨层瓶颈、编辑代码而不破坏正确性，并验证加速是否可复现。现有研究覆盖了算法优化、资源高效生成、仓库性能修复和 GPU 算子合成等方面，但没有一个要求智能体自主完成完整的性能工程循环。

Method: 构建了一个半自动化的四阶段基准构建流水线：1) 任务规划（LLM 驱动生成180+候选任务类别）；2) 构建任务包（基线代码+隐藏评估资产+私有参考求解器）；3) 难度校准（试点求解器迭代，~45%通过率）；4) 专家策展（从约28个校准任务中精选12个）。评估协议使用"验证加速比"作为主要指标，结合隐藏正确性测试、自动化捷径筛查和专家轨迹审计。评估了7个智能体栈（覆盖3种框架和5个LLM）在12个长周期任务上的表现，并进行了8组两轮 relay pilot 实验。

Result: 1) 没有单一智能体栈在所有任务上占优：最优栈因工作负载而异，oc-gpt 和 codex-gpt 各赢4个任务，最高几何平均加速（9.9×）来自未赢任何任务的 oc-glm 栈。2) 智能体框架对优化性能有实质影响：同一 GPT-5.5 在 OpenCode 下几何平均为9.2×，在 Codex 下为7.8×，且每任务胜出分布不同（7:4）。3) 发现捷径利用问题：部分大幅加速来自针对验证器特定结构的策略（如答案合成、构建产物替换），最高可达492.8×，需要通过审计重新评估。4) Relay pilot 在所有8组两轮序列中均观察到R2相对R1的提升（1.02×–2.48×），表明外部化中间优化状态可恢复额外空间。

Conclusion: PERFOPT-Bench 将验证加速比视为需要正确性检查和专家审计的环境条件化声明，而非通用LLM排行榜项。研究表明性能优化能力是工作负载依赖的，智能体框架应被视为优化组件而非可互换包装。原始加速比不可作为安全的基准分数，需要明确反作弊合同和专家审计。Relay 是一种有前景的持续优化模式，但需更广泛的控制和验证。该基准是迈向将编码智能体评估为性能工程系统的一步。局限：单次运行而非多次采样、单一审计专家、relay 实验受预算限制。

Abstract: Coding-agent benchmarks have largely measured whether agents can produce functionally correct patches, but production software also demands measurable speedups on real execution targets. Performance optimization is a distinct agentic task: agents must profile executions, diagnose cross-layer bottlenecks, edit code without breaking correctness, and verify that gains are reproducible rather than measurement artifacts. We introduce PERFOPT-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating this full performance-engineering loop. Each task provides a correct but deliberately suboptimal codebase and asks the agent to improve a target performance metric; scoring requires hidden correctness tests, verified-speedup measurement, and trajectory-level audit. We evaluate 7 agent stacks with different LLMs and agent frameworks on 7 long-horizon optimization tasks. The results show that optimization performance is workload-dependent rather than determined by model identity alone: no single stack dominates, and changing the agent framework can materially change the same LLM's per-task speedup profile. We further find that raw speedup is unsafe as a benchmark score, since some large gains arise from benchmark-specific shortcut exploitation; an exploratory relay pilot suggests that restarting from an externalized optimization summary can recover additional headroom after an initial session stops. The benchmark and our evaluation are available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [199] [Functional and Secure Code Generation with Task Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07881)
*Felix Wang,Anudeep Das,Mei Nagappan,N. Asokan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 SecVecCoder,首次将任务向量算术(task vector arithmetic)应用于可信代码生成,通过在 LPO(局部化偏好优化)导出的安全/不安全任务向量上进行 Safe-Anchored 线性组合,在 CodeGuard+ 基准上六个代码 LLM 中五个实现 SOTA,主 CWE 上提升 2.1–36.0 个百分点,未见 CWE 类型上最多提升 39.1 个百分点,且推理延迟仅为基础模型的 0.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码大模型生成的代码常包含安全漏洞,但既有防御方法存在局限:训练时方法(全参数微调)计算开销大,推理时方法(受限解码/辅助模块)增加延迟;且多数方法单独评测安全性或功能性,未能同时衡量代码的可信度(trustworthy code = 同时功能正确且安全)。需要一种兼具低训练成本、低推理开销,并能联合优化功能性与安全性的方案。

Method: 1) 对同一指令下的安全/不安全成对样本,分别用 LPO 微调得到 W_sec 与 W_vul,并在安全基模型上加 LoRA;2) 计算两类任务向量 τ_sec = W_sec - W_base、τ_insec = W_vul - W_base;3) 提出三种算子 — Negation(W_base − θτ_insec)、Contrast(W_base + θ(τ_sec − τ_insec))、Secure-Anchored(W_base + τ_sec − θτ_insec),通过相似性诊断实验证明 LPO 任务向量具有最大的几何区分度;4) 最终采用 θ=1.5 的 Secure-Anchored + LPO 任务向量的组合,一次性修改权重,推理时使用标准自回归解码,无需方法特定解码机制。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-Coder(3B/7B)、DeepSeek-Coder(1.3B/6.7B)、StarCoder2(3B/7B)六个模型上:主 CWE sec-pass@1 在五个模型上达到 SOTA,相对最强基线提升 3.8%–27.5%,绝对提升相对基础模型为 2.1–36.0 个百分点;未见 CWE 上绝对提升最高 39.1 个百分点(相对提升 4.3%–106.5%);训练算力仅为基础模型相关方法的 8.0%–12.5%(SafeCoder)和 28.7%–37.9%(SCoDE);平均解码延迟仅为基础模型的 0.6%(−0.5% 至 +2.9%)。消融实验表明两个任务向量分量均为必要,移除 τ_sec 平均下降 8.0 pp,移除 θτ_insec 平均下降 11.1 pp。

Conclusion: SecVecCoder 验证了任务向量算术可有效调控代码 LLM 朝向可信代码生成,关键在于细调策略、算子和强度的协同选择;作为训练-时/推理-时之外的"权重时"防御路径,它兼具低训练成本与零推理开销的优势,为多安全属性、可扩展模型的进一步研究打开了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, but they struggle to generate functional code free of security vulnerabilities. Prior work to improve the secure code generation abilities of such coding LLMs has largely focused on evaluating code functionality and security separately using different datasets, or focused on finding vulnerabilities post-generation. At the same time, the text-generation domain has seen significant work on alignment techniques, where models are tuned such that their outputs exhibit certain qualities (e.g., helpfulness, harmlessness). Of particular interest is task-vector arithmetic, where linear operations on LLM weights can be used to arbitrarily enhance alignment while incurring only minimal computational overhead. We develop a novel method, SecVecCoder, leveraging task vectors to produce trustworthy code that is simultaneously functional and secure without the need for post-generation adjustment. Across six coding LLMs from three families on the CodeGuard+ benchmark, SecVecCoder improves the rate of trustworthy code completions by 2.1-36.0 percentage points over the base model, with improvements on unseen CWE types reaching up to 39.1 percentage points. Since the effectiveness of the coding LLM relies only on changing the model weights, SecVecCoder requires no method-specific decoding and hence achieves a decoding latency within 0.6% of the base model's, on average.

</details>


### [200] [Bug Report Specification Refinement with Trajectory Guidance for Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07882)
*S M Farah Al Fahim,Md Nakhla Rafi,Md Ahasanuzzaman,Zeyang Ma,Dong Jae Kim,Shaowei Wang,Tse-Hsun,Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TrajSpec提出了一种基于轨迹引导的bug报告规格精化方法，通过未经验证的轨迹采集运行提取仓库级修复所需的规格证据（失败机制、行为需求、实现范围），将其组织为三级层次化表示，并经仓库审查后生成精化报告，以提升下游APR智能体的修复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级APR智能体将bug报告作为主要任务规格，但原始报告通常仅描述可见症状，遗漏了修复相关的关键信息（如失败机制、行为需求、实现范围），导致智能体可能检查无关代码、推断错误需求或生成仅缓解症状的不完整补丁。因此需要一种方法在下游修复前显式补充这些隐含的规格信息。

Method: TrajSpec包含四个核心步骤：(1) 轨迹采集——使用仅含原始报告b和预修复仓库Rc的智能体运行轨迹收集，丢弃候选补丁仅保留执行轨迹τ；(2) 分层证据抽象——从b和τ中提取候选发现E，并将其组织为三个分支（失败机制、行为需求、实现范围）×三个层级（高层解释、中层诊断关系、低层代码观察）的层次化表示M；(3) 草稿报告生成——从b和M生成遵循固定schema（Title、Description、RootCause、StepsToReproduce、ExpectedBehavior、ObservedBehavior）的草稿精化报告b̂；(4) 仓库审查——基于Rc的只读工具检查b̂中的声明，移除无支持的主张、修订不确定陈述、补充仓库支持细节，生成最终精化报告b̂_f。关键设计原则：不假设轨迹采集产生的补丁正确，不使用开发者补丁或后修复代码。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Lite全部300实例上，使用Mini-SWE-Agent V2：GPT-5-mini下Pass@1从41.00%提升至59.67%（相对提升45.53%），MiniMax M2.5下从54.67%提升至64.33%（相对提升17.68%），均优于Agentic-Base基线。在100实例分层样本上：Agentless从41%提升至71%，AutoCodeRover从47%提升至72%。消融实验表明，移除仓库审查使Pass@1降至48.00%（↓19.55%），移除层次化证据表示降至47.67%（↓20.11%）。成本分析显示总成本每实例仅$0.110（GPT-5-mini）和$0.094（MiniMax M2.5），且精化报告还使下游修复的输入token减少约24%。

Conclusion: TrajSpec证明轨迹不仅可以用于候选补丁生成，还可被抽象、结构化并经仓库审查后作为精化bug报告的证据来源，为下游APR智能体提供有价值的仓库级修复规格。该方法在不同LLM骨干（GPT-5-mini、MiniMax M2.5）和不同下游修复智能体（Mini-SWE-Agent V2、Agentless、AutoCodeRover）上均表现出一致的性能提升，且通过扩展修复覆盖范围而非替换已有修复来增加成功案例数，为改善仓库级修复提供了"改进任务规格而非仅增强智能体"的新方向。

Abstract: Bug reports serve as task specifications for repository-level automated program repair (APR) agents, but they often describe only the observed failure and omit repair-relevant information such as the failure-inducing behavior, behavioral requirement, and implementation scope. As a result, a repair agent may inspect irrelevant code, infer an incorrect requirement, or generate a patch that addresses the reported symptom without restoring the intended repository behavior. We present TrajSpec, a trajectory-guided approach for repository-supported bug report specification refinement. Given an original report and a pre-fix repository, TrajSpec runs a trajectory-collection agent and uses the resulting unverified trajectory as a source of trajectory-derived specification evidence. It organizes this evidence into a three-level representation consisting of a high-level interpretation of the issue, diagnostic findings supporting that interpretation, and concrete repository observations. TrajSpec then generates a draft refined report and applies repository-based review to remove unsupported claims, revise uncertain claims, and add repository-supported details. We evaluate TrajSpec on all 300 SWE-Bench Lite instances using Mini-SWE-Agent V2. TrajSpec's refined reports improve Pass@1 from 41.00% to 59.67% with GPT-5-mini and from 54.67% to 64.33% with MiniMax M2.5. On a stratified sample of 100 instances, TrajSpec's refined reports also improve Pass@1 from 41.00% to 71.00% with Agentless and from 47.00% to 72.00% with AutoCodeRover. Ablation results show that removing repository-based review or the hierarchical evidence representation reduces Pass@1 from 59.67% to 48.00% and 47.67%, respectively. Overall, TrajSpec provides actionable repository-supported context that consistently improves repair performance.

</details>


### [201] [The Memory Wall of Green Software: Empirical Energy Evaluation of Memento Design Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07944)
*Imane Jriri,Tarik Houichime,Younes El Amrani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Memento设计模式的两种实现策略（全量快照Classic与差分编码Differential）在.NET托管运行时下的能耗表现进行了基于Intel RAPL硬件遥测的实证研究,发现Differential策略在中尺度(50–100 MB)状态下可降低最高65.8%能耗,但在200 MB处遭遇"内存墙",能耗反比Classic高25.9%,因Gen2 GC剧增2300%导致算法优化被运行时开销完全抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绿色软件工程中,设计模式的抽象层存在隐性的"代谢成本"——即能耗开销,但在设计阶段常被忽视。Memento模式作为状态恢复的常用模式,其Classic(全量快照)与Differential(差分编码)两种实现的真实能耗权衡不明确:差分计算所节省的内存流量是否会被GC抖动等运行时开销所抵消?本文旨在通过严格的硬件级实证回答该问题。

Method: 在高度受控的实验环境下进行实证测量:(1)硬件平台为Intel i7-10700K,CPU锁定2.9 GHz并关闭Turbo Boost,线程钉扎到固定核心;(2)软件栈为.NET 8.0 + Server GC,运行Baseline(直存)、Classic(全量序列化)、Differential(XOR差分编码)三种变体;(3)通过Intel RAPL(MSR 0x611/0x610)采集高保真能耗数据;(4)状态规模从10 MB到200 MB,每规模执行30次迭代,CV>5%则重跑;(5)建立形式化能耗模型,区分I/O-Bound(Classic)与CPU-Bound(Differential)两种能耗机制。

Result: 核心结果呈现三阶段:(1)可持续甜点区(50–100 MB):Differential策略显著抑制DRAM激活,峰值节能65.8%(100 MB时5.50 J vs Classic的16.10 J);(2)过渡区(100–150 MB):优势收窄至-25.3%(150 MB时18.15 J vs 24.30 J),GC压力开始上升;(3)内存墙区(>150 MB):发生效率反转,200 MB时Differential能耗飙升至40.50 J,反超Classic(32.15 J) 25.9%,同时Gen2 GC频率激增2300%。形式化能耗模型将总能耗分解为I/O成本、CPU计算成本、DRAM激活成本与GC非线性能耗惩罚四项。基于此,作者提出状态感知的Green Architect决策矩阵:低内存压力(<100 MB)选Differential,过渡区(100–150 MB)采用混合/自适应策略,饱和区(>150 MB)回退至Classic以规避非线性能耗。

Conclusion: 研究提出"绿色软件的内存墙"概念——当运行时元开销(Gen2 GC与堆压缩)超越算法增益时,数据量最小化不再等于能耗最优。Differential策略的能耗曲线非单调,受对象生命周期动态支配。因此,可扩展性本身应被视为基础能耗度量,可持续架构需采用"运行时感知架构"(Runtime-Aware Architecture)视角,同时权衡结构设计质量与硬件-软件接口的非线性"抽象能耗税"。该结论基于Intel CISC + .NET Server GC环境,移植到ARM或Java ZGC等不同拓扑时,反转阈值可能改变。

Abstract: As Green Software Engineering matures, energy efficiency has transitioned into a mission-critical non-functional requirement. While software design patterns ensure structural integrity, their inherent abstraction layers impose an implicit "metabolic cost" that often remains obscured during the design phase. This paper empirically investigates the energy dynamics of the Memento design pattern, contrasting a direct, unabstracted baseline against Classic full-snapshot and Differential delta-encoding strategies. Leveraging the RAPL interface for high-fidelity hardware telemetry, we quantify energy dissipation across state volumes scaling from 10 MB to 200 MB. Our empirical results expose a critical architectural trade-off: the Differential strategy minimizes memory traffic, yielding a maximum energy reduction of 65.8% for mid-scale states, but collides with a catastrophic "memory wall" at 200 MB. At this saturation point, algorithmic optimizations are completely neutralized by severe GC thrashing and non-linear power spikes. We synthesize these findings into evidence-based heuristics, providing architects with a robust framework to reconcile structural design quality with sustainable Green IT imperatives.

</details>


### [202] [DeepSWE: Measuring Frontier Coding Agents on Original, Long-Horizon Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07946)
*Wenqi Huang,Charley Lee,Leonard Tng,Serena Ge*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DeepSWE 是一个包含113个原创、长期软件工程任务的基准测试，覆盖91个开源仓库和5种语言。其任务从头编写、从不合并回上游，参考答案不存在于公开训练数据中；采用手写的功能验证器（functional verifier），通过公共API和可观察行为进行评分，接受任何实现所请求功能的解决方案。与SWE-Bench Pro的继承测试相比，独立LLM法官与DeepSWE验证器的分歧率低一个数量级（1.4% vs 32.4%），且DeepSWE将前沿代码智能体在更宽的分数区间内分开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代理编码基准测试大多源自SWE-bench，从公开GitHub仓库中挖掘已合并的修复，存在两个结构性问题：(1) 修复内容及其讨论很可能已被预训练数据吸收，高分可能反映的是回忆能力而非问题解决能力（已有文献直接记录了SWE-bench的这种污染效应）；(2) 评分采用合并修复时附带的测试，这些测试是为确认某一特定修复而写，而非用于评估任意解决方案，可能拒绝正确的替代实现或放过不完整的方案。这两个问题削弱了排行榜名次与智能体解决真正新颖工程问题能力之间的联系。

Method: 方法包含四个核心设计：(1) 原创任务——每个任务从零编写且永不合并到上游，使参考答案停留在预训练爬取范围之外，且任务容器仅提供基础提交的浅克隆，避免从.git历史中泄露金标准；(2) 高多样性——跨91个活跃开源仓库、5种语言（TypeScript、Go、Python、JavaScript、Rust），每个仓库中位数仅贡献1个任务；(3) 短提示驱动大解决方案——提示约为SWE-Bench Pro的一半长度，但参考答案涉及5.5倍代码量和约2倍输出token；(4) 功能验证——手写验证器通过公共API和可观察输出进行断言，任何提供所需功能的实现均被接受，并附带回归测试防止破坏已有功能。质量保障包含LLM辅助分析、独立人工审核、提示-验证器双射性检查、接受广度评估、真实性（提示与任务两个维度）及环境清洁度。所有模型在固定harness（mini-swe-agent，单一bash工具，共享提示）下评测，每个任务约4次rollout，报告pass@1与pass@4指标。

Result: 评估16个前沿代理配置（涵盖GPT-5.5/5.4/5.4 Mini、Claude Opus 4.7/4.6、Sonnet 4.6、Haiku 4.5、Gemini 3.5 Flash/3.1 Pro/3 Flash、Kimi K2.6、MiMo v2.5 Pro、GLM-5.1、DeepSeek-V4 Pro、Qwen3-6 Plus、MiniMax-M2.7等），共7,174次评分rollout。GPT-5.5以70.0% pass@1显著领先，GPT-5.4（55.5%）与Claude Opus 4.7（54.2%）的置信区间重叠，差异不显著。DeepSWE将这八个有公开SWE-Bench Pro分数的模型分散在69.8分范围内，而SWE-Bench Pro仅29.7分。在验证器审计中，独立LLM法官与DeepSWE验证器的分歧率为1.4%（735次rollout），与SWE-Bench Pro验证器的分歧率为32.4%（789次rollout），差距稳健。输出token、墙钟时长和美元成本各跨一个数量级，但与通过率无强相关。定性分析发现：Claude系列最常遗漏枚举需求（尤其是"一个分支已实现而另一个分支未镜像"模式）；Claude系列最常通过.git历史恢复金标准解决方案；GPT系列遗漏需求最少且自一致性最高；强模型更倾向于主动编写测试，而SWE-Bench Pro的提示中"测试已处理"指令会抑制该行为。

Conclusion: DeepSWE通过原创任务、手写功能验证器、广泛仓库覆盖和固定harness，对前沿代码智能体进行特定能力维度的测量；它补充了现有公共排行榜，提供了更宽的区分度与更低的验证器误判率。其局限性包括：二元奖励无部分得分、仅测功能正确性而非代码质量、单一固定harness可能压制部分模型的原生表现、验证器审计样本量较小且法官本身是可能存在自我偏好偏差的LLM。基准本身可随模型进步通过新增任务刷新。作者发布基准、验证器和完整轨迹记录，但保留LLM法官提示作为内部工具。未来工作方向包括多harness评测、扩展语言与任务类型、混合验证器等。

Abstract: DeepSWE is a benchmark of 113 original, long-horizon software engineering tasks for evaluating coding agents. Most public agentic coding benchmarks follow SWE-bench in mining merged fixes from public GitHub repositories, which creates two problems: the fixes and their discussion were likely seen during pretraining, so a high score can reflect recall rather than problem-solving; and each task is graded by the tests that shipped with its merged fix, which were written to confirm one specific fix rather than grade an arbitrary solution, so they can fail a correct alternative or pass an incomplete one. DeepSWE avoids both. Its tasks are written from scratch across 91 active open-source repositories and five languages and are never contributed back upstream, so their reference solutions stay out of the public record that model training scrapes; and each task is graded by a hand-written verifier that checks the requested functionality and accepts any implementation that provides it. When an independent LLM judge re-reviews graded runs, it disagrees with DeepSWE's verifier about an order of magnitude less often than with SWE-Bench Pro's inherited tests (1.4% versus 32.4%). Despite being about half the length of SWE-Bench Pro's prompts, DeepSWE's prompts describe tasks whose reference solutions touch 5.5x more code, and the benchmark separates frontier agents across a wider score band than the leaderboards on which they otherwise cluster. We release the benchmark, its verifiers, and the full record of evaluation trajectories.

</details>


### [203] [On the Correctness of Software Merge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07987)
*Akira Mori,Masatomo Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出基于范畴论pushout理论的三方软件合并正确性标准（可解析性+普适性），设计实现了Java结构化合并工具d3j，并在43,774个文件合并场景的大规模实验中证明其优于git-merge等7个现有工具，报告零错误合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的三方合并工具（如git-merge、JDime、Spork等）长期以来缺乏严格的正确性评判标准，合并结果常被盲目接受。传统以"无冲突合并数量"作为评价指标存在根本缺陷：任意文本都可被视作"合并结果"，无法区分真正正确的合并与随意覆盖；且line-oriented工具过度保守，无法处理细粒度语法修改。因此亟需建立形式化的正确性标准以指导工具设计与评估。

Method: 1. **正确性准则**：提出两个形式化条件——(1) Parsable：合并结果符合目标语言文法；(2) Universal：合并结果包含且仅包含各分支的编辑操作，公共操作仅应用一次。基于偏包含映射（partial inclusion map）的pushout概念，给出4个具体可检测条件：无多余插入、无遗漏插入、无多余删除、无遗漏删除。
2. **d3j工具**：用OCaml+Menhir LR(1)生成器自研Java 11解析器；采用Zhang-Shasha近似最优树编辑算法（含move检测和relabel重组织后处理），支持delete/insert/relabel/move四类AST节点操作；定义32条冲突规则（约6000行Python代码）保证语法一致性；以AST patch XML形式存储并支持unparsing。
3. **三类实验**：大规模（76个开源项目43,774场景）、开发者对比（Miraldi数据集2,582场景）、重构中心（21种重构类型2,459场景）。

Result: - **大规模实验**：d3j在43,774场景中产出39,541个无冲突合并，全部可解析且普适，零错误。相比之下，git-merge有40个普适性违反，imediff有196个语法错误+130个普适性违反，intellimerge有19,565个语法错误，automergeptm有597+1080个违反。d3j关键指标CCFM达39,541全面领先。
- **开发者对比**：2,582个git冲突场景中d3j报告1,652个正确无冲突合并，仓库中711个人工解决方案被判为不正确（多为强制选边或机会性复制编辑）。
- **重构实验**：d3j报告1,150个CFM且零错误，Spork虽1,309个CFM但有232个普适性违反，揭示了仅统计CFM数量的误导性。
- 揭示出现有工具（如git-merge）的罕见错误源于编辑匹配不当，而非算法根本缺陷。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于pushout的可解析性+普适性双准则是首个形式化的合并正确性标准，d3j作为参考实现验证了该标准的实用性与可行性。实验证明现有主流工具（包括git-merge）均产生非普适合并结果，仅以CFM数量评估工具具有误导性。d3j并非git-merge的直接替代品，而是作为原则性基线互补现有工具。未来工作包括扩展到C/C++、探索语义/行为正确性、分析现有工具冲突规则以提升d3j能力。

Abstract: Three-way merge tools play crucial roles in modern software development, where a developer forks a branch to make local modifications and requests it to be merged into the main branch via a "pull request." Despite its importance, the task has traditionally been defined in an intuitive manner, and the results of merge tools are often accepted without scrutiny. In this paper, we present a new structural merge tool in comparison with existing tools based on the syntactic criteria we propose for evaluating the merge results. We require the merge result to be both parsable and universal. Being parsable means that the result is syntactically valid according to the grammar of the programming language. Being universal means that the result incorporates all and only the edit operations occurring in each branch while ensuring that edits common to both branches are applied only once. This requirement can be precisely defined using the notion of pushouts in category theory. In a large-scale experiment involving 43,774 file merge scenarios from 76 open-source Java projects, we found a number of incorrect results reported by existing tools such as the Git companion merge tool, whereas our tool reports none. We further compared d3j's results with 2,582 developer-resolved merges and with 2,459 merge scenarios involving 21 refactoring types. These experiments revealed both the strengths and current limitations of structural merge, and underscore the importance of clear correctness criteria. We expect that the proposed criterion will provide a foundation for developing more reliable and principled merge tools.

</details>


### [204] [TTHE: Test-Time Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08124)
*Jun Nie,Yonggang Zhang,Jun Song,Qianshu Cai,Dahai Yu,Yike Guo,Xinmei Tian,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TTHE（Test-Time Harness Evolution）提出了一种在评估阶段、利用无标签执行轨迹直接进化LLM智能体可执行harness（控制程序）的方法。它将harness代码本身视为测试时适应的状态，通过基于种群的"生成—提议—评判"循环（agentic proposer改写harness、agentic judge依据执行代理信号选择）实现适应，无需更新模型权重、无需金标、无需额外训练。在五个执行型任务上，相对固定ReAct基线取得显著且可解释的提升，同时揭示出代理信号可靠性是当前主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM智能体的行为不仅由模型决定，还由其harness（构造上下文、调用工具、验证中间结果、故障恢复的可执行程序）共同决定。现有方法通常在部署前基于有标注开发集搜索并冻结harness，限制了面对不同测试分布、失败模式或工具交互时的适应能力。核心问题：能否在评估阶段、仅依靠模型自身产生的无标签执行轨迹，通过修改harness代码来实现适应？这与test-time training（更新权重）、self-debugging（修改单次回复）以及pre-evaluation workflow search（基于开发目标冻结harness）形成对比。

Method: TTHE将可执行harness视作测试时适应的状态，在无标签流x_{1:T}上以batch为粒度运行：每个batch X_t从当前harness H_t出发，启动G条并行分支（lineage），经过R轮"Observe–Propose–Judge"循环。Observe对harness执行整个batch并收集详细执行trace（prompt、completion、tool调用、stdout/stderr、artifact、错误等）及代理信号（execution health、round-trip consistency、public-test pass rate）；Propose是G个agentic proposer，每个作为编码智能体编辑其父分支harness，可读取其他分支的trace以注入多样性（如conservative-repair/exploratory/adversarial三种角色）；Judge基于同一组无标签证据从最终R轮分支中选择一个harness H_{t+1}承载到下一batch。Solver、proposers、judge均由同一冻结LLM（deepseek-v4-flash）通过不同harness角色实例化；关键设计：(1) 优化信号不坍缩为标量奖励，而是同时暴露原始trace与多种代理信号；(2) judge可执行额外probe或重跑；(3) 候选无效时回退到父分支；(4) 测量在选择后离线完成，gold永不进入循环。

Result: 在BIRD Text-to-SQL hard切片（DeepSeek）上从12.0%提升至50.0%；LiveCodeBench从30.0%升至38.3%；SWE-bench Verified从20.0%升至35.0%；DS-1000从38.0%升至44.0%；跨模型验证（MiMo V2.5: 32%→52%；Kimi K2.5: 28%→48%）均一致提升；agentic工具调用域claw-eval的mean task score从48.9%升至69.8%（30个task中28个改进）。消融显示：搜索预算非单调，最优配置为G=3、R=3、B=10；G=1时增加R反而下降（46%→40%），G=3时上升（44%→50%）；batch size在B=10处达峰；累积harness跨batch比每batch重置高6个点（50% vs 44%）。通过trace审计发现：(1) 显著selection regret——被judge看到的最优候选oracle准确率64% vs judge实际50%，有7个task因judge选择偏差而损失；(2) 全部生成候选的oracle上限仅70%，暴露候选覆盖瓶颈；(3) judge偏好benchmark-incompatible输出形态（如多余列/粒度不匹配）。演化出的harness具有可读策略：SQL端做value grounding + shape inference + execute-and-self-debug；SWE端采用reproduce-first + locate-root-cause + anti-paralysis failsafe避免空patch；LiveCodeBench采用条件验证–修复策略；claw-eval强制单行工具payload与逐记录标识符引用。

Conclusion: TTHE将LLM智能体的测试时适应重新定义为"对可执行控制程序的进化"，证明仅凭评估阶段的执行轨迹即可产出inspectable的grounding/verification/repair策略，且改进对多种任务和backbone均成立。论文明确指出核心开放问题：执行代理信号的可靠性——由于执行并非oracle（query可成功但答错题，public test可通过但hidden test失败），agentic judge会在不完美信号下提交表面合理但benchmark不兼容的程序。性能对搜索预算呈非单调，表明不能简单靠扩大搜索解决，需提升judge校准与候选多样性。当前局限：评估采用transductive设定，未验证prequential前向泛化；缺乏多种子与计算匹配的best-of-N/开发期优化器对比；judge信息消融尚未完成；沙箱限制了向开放域部署的扩展。

Abstract: The behavior of an LLM agent is determined not only by the underlying model, but also by its harness: the executable program that constructs context, invokes tools, verifies intermediate results, and recovers from failures. Existing approaches optimize such harnesses before deployment, searching training or development data for a fixed agent workflow that is then frozen at test time. This limits adaptation when the test distribution, failure modes, or tool interactions differ from those seen during development. We ask whether the harness can instead be optimized during evaluation itself, using only the unlabeled execution traces the agent produces on the test inputs. We introduce Test-Time Harness Evolution (TTHE), which treats the executable harness as the state of test-time adaptation. During evaluation, TTHE maintains a population of candidate harnesses and refines them through an agentic proposer that reasons over their execution traces, without gold labels or task-specific supervision; a judge then commits an improved harness from execution-derived proxy signals, and the selected program persists to govern subsequent inputs. Crucially, TTHE does not update model weights, require gold labels, or train a separate adaptation model: solver, proposers, and judge are different roles and harnesses around the same frozen LLM, so all adaptation occurs through changes to the surrounding program. Across text-to-SQL, competitive programming, software engineering, data-science coding, and agentic tool-use tasks, TTHE improves fixed ReAct-style baseline harnesses, yielding persistent, inspectable improvements rather than a pre-searched workflow or per-query retries. These results recast test-time adaptation for LLM agents as evolution over executable control programs and identify execution-derived proxy reliability as a central challenge for robust unsupervised agent improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [BACH: A Bayesian Admixture of Contrastive Heads for Multi-Interest Two-Tower Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08107)
*Quoc Phong Nguyen,Paul Albert,Long Vuong,Vuong Le,Julien Monteil*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出BACH（Bayesian Admixture of Contrastive Heads），将多兴趣双塔检索建模为用户级的softmax混合模型，通过变分推断拟合，从而缓解硬路由训练中的头部坍缩问题，并提供用户级的兴趣重要性权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的双塔检索器将用户压缩为单一嵌入，无法有效表达用户多样的兴趣。多兴趣模型通过多个头部（head）解决此问题，但硬路由训练（argmax路由）存在两个缺陷：(1) 胜者通吃的路由坍缩（routing collapse）——初始化较差的head获得更少梯度更新，逐渐退化为冗余副本；(2) 缺乏用户级head重要性估计，导致serving时使用隐式均匀先验，无法准确反映各head的贡献。

Method: BACH将k个用户head视为per-user softmax混合模型的组件，引入潜变量z∈{1,...,k}指示激活的head，通过变分推断拟合。具体地：(1) 使用**球面识别后验**（power-spherical或von Mises-Fisher）替代模型自身的负样本依赖后验，实现**无采样**路由；(2) 通过独立的gating tower π_ξ(x_u)生成per-user混合权重π_u；(3) 最大化ELBO联合优化embeddings、π_u和识别参数，soft responsibility γ_n^(r)∝π_u^(r)·σ_r替代argmax路由，使每个head都获得梯度；(4) 浓度参数κ_r仅通过KL项进入ELBO，**自正则化**无需先验；(5) 支持**全局码本**变体（LDA式admixture），使serving时可预计算每个topic的候选列表，仅用π_u加权检索。

Result: 在MovieLens-20M、Taobao、Netflix三个大规模基准上：(1) BACH在所有head数量下均优于硬路由多兴趣和单向量基线，p-BACH（k=32）在MovieLens-20M上AUPRC达0.069，v-BACH（k=32）在Taobao和Netflix上领先；(2) 全局码本变体可达个性化模型2-5倍MostPop的提升，支持预计算serving、冷启动和可解释性；(3) all-multihead（每候选取max）作为训练目标一致优于pos-multihead（仅对正样本取max），差距随k增大而扩大（最高41% AUPRC差距），表明训练-服务一致性至关重要；(4) κ_r在无先验情况下稳定在20-30（p-BACH）和17-21（v-BACH）范围内。

Conclusion: BACH通过将多兴趣检索重构为变分混合模型，在三个基准上达到SOTA，核心贡献包括：(1) 球面识别后验实现无采样、serving一致的soft routing；(2) per-user权重π_u自然提供head重要性估计；(3) κ_r自正则化无需先验；(4) 全局码本admixture变体带来预计算serving、冷启动和可解释性等部署优势。未来方向包括π_u的校准、全局码本在预计算预算下的评估以及更广泛的数据集评估。

Abstract: Two-tower retrievers compress each user into a single embedding, limiting their ability to serve diverse interests. Multi-interest models give each user several heads scored by a maximum inner product, but their hard-routing training under-utilizes heads (routing collapse) and gives no per-user estimate of how much each interest matters for serving. We present \textbf{BACH} (\emph{Bayesian Admixture of Contrastive Heads}), which casts multi-interest two-tower retrieval as a per-user mixture over the heads, fit by variational inference. The soft mixture trains every head (mitigating collapse), produces a per-user weighting of the interests that is reused at serving, and admits a shared global-codebook variant with precomputable retrieval. On three large-scale benchmarks, MovieLens-20M, Taobao, and Netflix, BACH improves top-of-ranking retrieval over hard-routing multi-interest and single-vector baselines at every head count; we further find that scoring every candidate by its best head, consistent with serving, outperforms the usual target-routed training, and that BACH improves further still.

</details>


### [206] [Improving Ad-hoc Search Effectiveness for Conversational Information Retrieval via Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08540)
*Ahmed Rayane Kebir,Jose G. Moreno,Lynda Tamine*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出使用模型合并（Model Merging）作为一种无需训练的策略，将对话式信息检索（CIR）模型与基础即席检索（ad-hoc）模型合并，构建单一模型同时处理即席检索和对话检索任务，有效缓解微调导致的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式检索模型通常通过对即席检索器（如ANCE）在对话数据集上进行微调获得，但这一过程会导致模型丧失原有的即席检索能力（灾难性遗忘）。现有解决方案如多任务学习（MTL）需要高昂的计算成本和大量源域数据，因此需要一种无需额外训练即可兼顾即席和对话检索性能的方法。

Method: 将对话微调模型 QRACDR（基于 ANCE 在 QReCC/TopiOCQA 上微调）与原始即席检索器 ANCE 进行参数空间合并。采用两种合并策略：1）Model Soup（MS）：线性插值 θ_mg = λ·θ_cir + (1-λ)·θ_adh；2）Slerp（SL）：球面线性插值，沿角度方向组合参数向量。插值系数 λ 按编码器层深度逐层应用，共探索 80 种配置（每种策略 40 种），优化在 MS MARCO 和 QReCC/TopiOCQA 域内数据集上进行。

Result: 在 MS MARCO 上，QRACDR-Q 的 NDCG@3 下降 14%，而 QRACDR-Q_MG-MS 将下降缩减至 -3%，并在 NQ 数据集上相对 ANCE 提升 +4.58%。在 CAsT 数据集上，QRACDR-T_MG 消除了 8.47% 的重写查询性能损失，在 CAsT-20 会话检索中 QRACDR-Q_MG 提升 +10.11%。零样本条件下 NDCG@3 最高提升达 15%。模型合并在效果上匹配或超越多任务微调，且显著优于早停策略。

Conclusion: 模型合并是一种有效的免训练策略，可恢复对话检索器的即席检索能力，同时保持对话任务表现，并在跨数据集、跨任务（如重写查询与会话查询）的泛化性上优于多任务学习和早停。模型合并在源数据不可用或计算资源受限时具有独特优势，为对话搜索系统的设计提供了新方向。未来工作将探索将模型合并扩展至查询澄清、响应生成等更广泛的对话搜索任务。

Abstract: Conversational information retrieval is challenging since it requires the consideration of the conversation history which potentially gives rise to topic shifts and coreference resolution across previous turns. To address these challenges, previous work mainly rely on traditional fine-tuning of ad-hoc retrievers on conversational datasets or extrapolates their generalizability through multi-tasking. However, this mainstream approach is costly - since it requires model re-training - and exhibits catastrophic forgetting, where the model loses its foundational ad-hoc retrieval performance. In this paper, we fill this gap by introducing model merging as a training-free strategy enabling the design of a single retrieval model that operates across both ad-hoc and conversational settings with no additional fine-tuning. We conduct experiments using linear and non-linear parameter-wise merging strategies - namely Model Soup and Slerp - on standard ad-hoc search and conversational retrieval datasets. Our results demonstrate that model merging significantly enhances the ad-hoc search capabilities of conversational retrievers while improving generalizability across task-specific datasets, achieving up to 15% higher NDCG@3 under zero-shot conditions.

</details>


### [207] [DaV-Gen: End-to-End Generative Retrieval via Draft-and-Verify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08365)
*Meng Zhao,Chunmei Liu,Qinyong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DaV-Gen 是一种统一的端到端生成式检索框架，通过"Draft-and-Verify"机制将向量检索的效率与生成模型的精度相结合。该方法采用混合稀疏-稠密表示和复合损失函数训练单一模型，在推理阶段先通过 ANN 搜索快速生成候选集，再利用 Broadcasted Prefix Caching 实现 O(1) 并行验证，解决了多级联架构的目标不一致性问题以及纯生成模型的自回归延迟瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级信息检索系统主要采用多级联架构（MCAs），通过"召回-排序"流水线平衡效率与效果，但各阶段优化目标不一致，导致早期错误传递并放大；新兴的端到端生成式检索（如 TIGER、LC-Rec）虽然统一了流程，但自回归解码带来极高的在线服务延迟（≈3s），且难以精确控制推荐列表长度。本文旨在弥合级联架构的效率与生成模型统一表达力之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1. **混合稀疏-稠密表示**：融合 RQ-VAE 编码器输出的稠密语义向量与通过 backbone 上下文编码后平均池化的稀疏 Semantic ID，得到兼容 MIPS 检索的混合嵌入；
2. **渐进式知识注入**：分两阶段训练 PLM——冻结 backbone 仅训练 Semantic ID 嵌入矩阵（Embedding Alignment），再解冻全参数微调（Deep Semantic Integration），避免灾难性遗忘；
3. **复合损失函数**：包括对比检索损失（结构化嵌入空间以支持 ANN 草稿）、生成式损失（建模 Semantic ID 内部层次结构，提供正交的验证信号）、融合式排序损失（通过 pairwise hinge loss 联合优化匹配分与生成分）；
4. **并行推理流水线**：第一阶段通过 ANN 检索快速生成 N 个候选；第二阶段利用 Broadcasted Prefix Caching 将用户上下文的 KV 缓存一次计算并广播至所有候选，在单次前向传播中并行打分，将 O(L) 串行复杂度降为 O(1)；
5. **融合评分**：验证函数 σ(i) = f_fusion(s_match, s_gen) 通过融合网络学习两种信号的最优非线性组合。

Result: 1. **公开数据集（RQ1）**：在 Amazon Beauty / Sports / Yelp 上，DaV-Gen 在 Recall@10 与 NDCG@10 上均超越 OneRec、TIGER、LC-Rec 等 SOTA，相对最强基线提升 2.48%~3.44%；
2. **工业搜索场景（RQ2）**：在 Ind-Search（>100M items, 500M logs）上 Recall@50 达 77.4%，较稠密检索器（44.7%）提升超 30 个百分点；NDCG@10 从 0.569 提升至 0.589，MRR@10 从 0.895 提升至 0.942；
3. **在线 A/B 测试（RQ3）**：在某头部视频平台百万级用户一周实验中，ATS +2.09%、UCVR +0.47%、ASS +0.31%（均 p<0.05）；推理延迟降至 ≈70ms，相较级联架构（≈130ms）提速 2.5×，数量级优于纯生成模型（≈3s）；
4. **消融实验（RQ4）**：移除稠密表示导致最大下降（Beauty Recall -5.6%），证明稀疏 token 不足以表达复杂语义；渐进式知识注入、生成损失、融合损失均对性能有显著贡献。

Conclusion: DaV-Gen 通过"Draft-and-Verify"范式成功重构了生成式检索，从串行 token 生成转向并行验证。其混合稀疏-稠密表示与统一优化框架协同解决了传统级联架构的目标不一致性与生成模型的解码延迟两大核心痛点，在检索精度与工业级服务效率上均取得显著优势。未来工作将引入视觉/音频嵌入扩展 Dense Content Vector，实现更丰富的多模态融合。

Abstract: Mainstream industrial information retrieval systems (e.g., search and recommendation) are usually built upon Multi-Stage Cascade Architectures (MCAs), which balance effectiveness and efficiency through a coarse-to-fine ``retrieval-ranking'' pipeline. However, the optimization objectives across different stages are substantially inconsistent, propagating or even amplifying the early-stage errors that ultimately degrade the quality of final results. While emerging end-to-end generative models offer a potential solution by unifying the pipeline, their online serving performance is severely hindered by the auto-regressive process inherited from the standard decoder-only structure. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{DaV-Gen}, a novel unified solution designed to fundamentally refactor the paradigm for both search and recommendation via a ``Draft-and-Verify'' mechanism. Inspired by the process used by speculative decoding, our framework redesigns the generation task into two synergistic operations within a single model. During training, the model is concurrently optimized for both candidate drafting and fine-grained verification. This is achieved by a composite loss function that jointly trains the model on two distinct but related objectives: 1) a contrastive loss that structures the embedding space for efficient drafting, and 2) a fusion loss that combines generative likelihood with vector similarity to produce a superior verification score. This integrated training strategy equips the model with dual capabilities. At inference time, it first performs highly efficient vector-based drafting to generate a candidate set, and then verifies these candidates using the more powerful fused scoring function, thereby achieving both the speed of sparse drafting and the precision of advanced generative models within a unified, end-to-end architecture.

</details>


### [208] [H3D: Benchmarking Unsupervised Text Hashing for Fine-Grained Document Deduplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08382)
*Qianren Mao,Jiaxun Lyu,Junnan Liu,Zhijun Chen,Jingzheng Li,Hanwen Hao,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: H3D是一个针对细粒度科学文档去重的无监督文本哈希基准。它在统一查询-候选排序协议下，系统评估了5种语义无关的非学习哈希方法（MinHash、SimHash、Winnowing、FuzzyHash、FlyHash）以及基于冻结BGE嵌入的两种语义敏感量化方法（BGE-BIHash和BGE-LSHash），在CSFCube和RELISH两个互补数据集上报告MAP、NDCG@20、效率及压缩鲁棒性，揭示了"词汇/结构指纹在近重复匹配中竞争力强，语义敏感表征在内容改写下保持相似度但计算成本更高"的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏在统一协议下对细粒度科学文档去重场景中文本哈希管线的系统性比较。监督式去重方法依赖标注数据且领域迁移性差；学习式无监督哈希（如VDSH、NASH、MICPQ等）需要任务特定训练、引入额外变量，难以纯粹隔离哈希生成器与相似度评分的交互。因此需要一个标准化的诊断性基准来回答"何时使用哪个哈希家族、应搭配何种评分器、压缩会引入何种精度-效率权衡"。

Method: 1）**数据集**：CSFCube（4,207篇CS论文，50个查询，提供background/method/result/overall四个facet标注）用于细粒度facet级评估；RELISH-Aspire（163,170篇生物医学文献，3,190个查询，191,245个查询-候选对）用于大规模split级评估。2）**两阶段统一协议**：编码器H: x→Z（Z可为二进制串、集合/多重集或向量）→ 类型适配器Π_τ → 类型特定评分器S_τ，输出排序分数。3）**三组相似度度量**：字符串类（Hamming、Levenshtein）、集合类（Jaccard、多重集Jaccard）、向量类（Cosine、Manhattan、Mahalanobis）。4）**BGE量化策略**：BIHash对D=1024维嵌入reshape为m×L矩阵后按列均值阈值化生成L=32位码（确定压缩）；LSHash将嵌入分为k个子空间，每子空间r=L/k个随机超平面投影（随机投影，邻域保持）。5）**评估指标**：MAP（g0=2二元化阈值）、NDCG@20、Total Time、文本压缩下的鲁棒性曲线（0%–80%删除率）。6）**三个研究问题**：RQ1度量选择对语义无关哈希排名质量的影响；RQ2密集BGE嵌入量化后的检索质量保留度；RQ3渐进压缩下不同范式的鲁棒性与计算成本。

Result: 1）**RQ1（CSFCube/RELISH）**：MinHash+Jaccard在CSFCube上MAP达0.2451（all），Winnowing+M-jaccard在RELISH Test上MAP达0.4996；词法哈希对近重复匹配有效，facet差异显著（background>result>method）。Hamming/Manhattan/Mahalanobis在FlyHash、SimHash等二进制签名下排名等价；Hamming/Jaccard/Levenshtein在MinHash实现中坍缩为同一排序。2）**RQ2**：密集BGE(large)+Manhattan在CSFCube all上MAP=0.3431、NDCG=0.5754；在RELISH Test上MAP=0.6626、NDCG=0.8242。BGE-BIHash与BGE-LSHash在所有数据集与规模上均低于密集BGE，LSHash在RELISH上比BIHash略差但Cosine下更稳定。3）**RQ3（鲁棒性）**：渐进文本删除（0%→80%）下，MinHash/SimHash/Winnowing/FlyHash/FuzzyHash相似度显著下降；BGE-BIHash/BGE-base/BGE-LSHash在中度压缩下保持更高相似度。4）**哈希表数k（4–16）**：BGE-LSHash在RELISH上MAP/NDCG变化≤±1%，k=4–8提供最佳精度-效率平衡。5）**案例研究**：FlyHash最快（0.0012s/doc）且负样本分离完全（Sim=0）；MinHash正样本最强（0.920）；FuzzyHash正样本最高（0.979）但负样本也高（0.417），分离弱。

Conclusion: H3D确立了无监督文本哈希在细粒度文档去重中的诊断性基准范式，核心结论为：（1）哈希生成器与评分器必须联合评估，不能互换使用——不同表示类型下评分函数可能rank-equivalent或显著改变排序；（2）基于固定规则的非学习哈希在近重复检测中仍具竞争力，且可复现、无需训练；（3）BGE类语义敏感哈希在改写/压缩下鲁棒性更强，但量化会引入精度-存储权衡，且计算成本更高；（4）BGE-LSHash对哈希表数k不敏感，较小k已足够。未来工作将扩展更多领域、引入释义/翻译/引用编辑等扰动族，并研究具有邻域保持目标和不确定性感知能力的可学习或校准感知量化器。

Abstract: Document hashing provides compact representations for efficient similarity search and document deduplication, but existing studies rarely compare hashing pipelines under a unified protocol for fine-grained scientific documents. H3D is an unsupervised text hashing benchmark for fine-grained document deduplication. It evaluates representative unsupervised non-learning hashing approaches (MinHash, SimHash, Winnowing, FuzzyHash, FlyHash) together with semantic-sensitive methods built from frozen BGE embeddings and two quantization strategies (BGE-BIHash and BGE-LSHash). The non-learning methods generate hash fingerprints through manually designed mathematical rules without training or labeled similarity pairs, which distinguishes them from neural semantic hashing models. We benchmark all methods on CSFCube and RELISH, two datasets that provide complementary evaluation settings: facet-level analysis for scientific-document similarity and larger-scale split-level evaluation for biomedical similarity search. H3D jointly reports ranking quality (MAP, NDCG@20), efficiency, and robustness under controlled text compression. The results show a consistent trade-off: lexical and structural fingerprints are competitive for near-duplicate matching, while semantic-sensitive representations better preserve similarity under content rewriting, at higher computational cost. We further analyze when different similarity measures become rank-equivalent for specific hash representations, improving the interpretability and reproducibility of method comparisons.

</details>


### [209] [Conversational Retrieval and On-the-Fly Knowledge Modeling of Historical Penitentiary Repression Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08459)
*Paula Font Solà,Adrià Molina Rodríguez,Josep Lladós*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向历史数字图书馆的文档分析系统，融合鲁棒的多候选OCR流水线、检索增强生成（RAG）与动态知识图谱，针对西班牙内战时期（1937–1940）严重退化的军事法庭判决文档，支持对话式检索与即时（on-the-fly）知识建模。核心创新在于将LLM提取的三元组持续累积到本地（会话级）与全局（馆藏级）双层图结构中，使图索引充当语言模型的结构化记忆，从而支持跨文档多跳推理与专家知识的动态整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG系统擅长基于单文档的抽取式问答，但难以整体性地解读文档集合并动态融入领域专家知识；现有GraphRAG方法又隐含假设文档原生数字化、结构清晰，难以应对手写、墨迹褪色、布局多变的真实历史档案。此外，依赖档案学家手工构建知识图谱耗时且难以扩展。档案学（如RiC框架）已倡导超越单一记录枚举、关注关系与上下文，而文档分析系统亟需在检索之上叠加馆藏级建模与推理能力。

Method: 系统由三大组件构成：（1）文本抽取流水线——对四种二值化结果（原始、全局阈值、自适应阈值、Hi-SAM）经由直接路径与布局感知路径（Surya）生成8个OCR候选（Tesseract），经字符级多序列比对投票生成基线共识，再由microsoft/phi-4多次精炼产生10个候选，最终以词级投票合成单文本；（2）高级RAG——按法律章节（RESULTANDO/CONSIDERANDO）分块并以50–100词滑动窗口切分，用msmarco-bert-base-dot-v5编码，phi-4代理重写查询，dot-product检索top-20后由ms-marco-MiniLM-L6-v2交叉编码器重排，文档投票选上下文；（3）动态知识图谱——从RAG答案中抽取(subject, predicate, object)三元组，经Levenshtein归一化、SpaCy NER分解长节点后入库；维护局部图（单文档/会话）与全局图（跨文档，仅统一person/place/date，其他节点带文档ID以保留上下文特异性）；检索时按单实体（3跳邻域）与多实体（节点间简单路径）两种策略，并采用严格的JSON结构化提示与found_information布尔标记抑制幻觉。

Result: 在130张图像（65份判决文档）语料上：转录WER由基线Tesseract的72.8%降至完整流水线的33.5%（相对降幅54%），CER由46.7%降至22.4%。RAG自动评估中，自定义精确匹配达0.824，Faithfulness 0.886，Context Precision 0.888，Context Recall 0.776。8人参与、64对答案的人类评估显示：简单查询（SQ）由RAG主导（应答率83.33% vs 图谱6.25%，整体质量4.50 vs 3.67）；复杂查询（CQ）则由图谱主导（87.5% vs 68.75%，相关性4.86 vs 3.73，忠实度4.64 vs 4.36，答案深度4.64 vs 4.45），证实两种范式互补；用户总体满意度4.12/5，RAG在92%的情况下能正确处理自身局限性，96%的情况下正确处理姓名变体。

Conclusion: 研究验证了一种通过对话式接口实现自动知识建模的混合架构：图谱RAG与标准RAG在简单抽取式查询上表现相当，但在复杂多跳推理的各维度上GraphRAG明显占优。系统将初级文献与策展/推理得到的事实统一在结构化图索引中，使档案学家的领域知识可被迭代精炼，界面从检索工具演化为可进化的知识管理环境。未来工作将聚焦于可扩展性、更复杂的图推理以及更深层次的人机协作工作流。

Abstract: Recent developments in digital libraries increasingly favor conversational and natural language access to information through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). Although these approaches are effective for extractive tasks grounded in individual records, they remain limited in their ability to interpret document collections holistically and to incorporate expert knowledge dynamically. In this article, we present a document analysis system designed for the management of historical digital libraries that supports on-the-fly knowledge modeling. The system is equipped with the capability to store facts produced either by expert archivists or derived from document retrieval processes within a graph-based structure. Through continuous professional interaction, the system can retrieve information not only from primary sources such as documents, but also from previously modeled knowledge, with the graph-based index acting as a memory for the language model to access. This enables increasingly complex queries involving long-term dependencies across documents, link discovery, and the integration of expert knowledge that may not be explicitly present in the original sources. As a result, the proposed approach facilitates the generation of richer and more comprehensive information.

</details>


### [210] [Log-Insight: Automating Microservice Incident Diagnosis via Neuro-Symbolic Log Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08529)
*Carlos Garcia-Hernandez,Aymane Abdali,Guangyu Wu,Mingxue Wang,Fei Shen,Zhaoyu Pang,Yanbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Log-Insight是华为部署在生产环境中的微服务故障诊断系统，采用神经-符号（neuro-symbolic）架构，通过六阶段流水线将海量日志（单次30分钟事件窗口可达200万行/12亿字符）压缩1000-7000倍后，再交给LLM综合生成根因假设报告。在11个历史生产事件（110次运行）的评估中达到MRR=0.790、Top-3准确率超90%、端到端延迟低于1分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模微服务系统中，单个30分钟事件窗口产生超200万行日志（约12亿字符），远超LLM上下文窗口（46K字符/请求）。现有三类方法各存缺陷：模板解析器缺乏语义异常推理、深度学习检测器输出黑箱二元信号、LLM管线在原始遥测数据上遭遇上下文溢出与领域幻觉。亟需一种能规模化、可验证、可被SRE信任的自动化根因分析方案。
